### [1] [An Elementary Proof of Hopf's Curvatura Integra Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26294)
*Pavel Grinfeld*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文利用移动曲面微积分(Calculus of Moving Surfaces)给出了Hopf曲率积分定理关键方面的初等证明，证明了超曲面的总曲率（高斯-克罗内克曲率B的曲面积分）与超曲面的形状无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hopf的curvatura integra定理是微分几何中的一个重要定理，其原有证明可能较为复杂。作者希望提供一个更初等、更直接的证明，以展示总曲率不依赖于超曲面形状这一核心性质。

Method: 作者使用移动曲面微积分(Calculus of Moving Surfaces)这一工具，在超曲面的平滑形状变化下计算总曲率（即高斯-克罗内克曲率B的曲面积分）的变化率，并证明该变化率为零。

Result: 证明了在超曲面的光滑形变下，总曲率的变化率恒为零，从而给出了Hopf curvatura integra定理关键方面的初等证明。

Conclusion: 通过移动曲面方法，作者给出了一个更简洁初等的证明路径，确认了总曲率是超曲面的拓扑不变量，与几何形状无关。

Abstract: We provide an elementary proof of the key aspect of Hopf's curvatura integra theorem. Namely, we show that the total curvature, i.e. the surface integral of the Gauss-Kronecker curvature B, is independent of the shape of the hypersurface. We accomplish this task by using the Calculus of Moving Surfaces to show that the rate of change of the total curvature vanishes under smooth changes in shape.

</details>


### [2] [A Horizon-Free Extrinsic Penrose Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26565)
*Caiyan Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了三维欧氏空间中适当嵌入的平均凸平面曲面S（具有有限多个末端）的质量不等式：m_+(S) ≥ √(A_S/π)，其中m_+(S)为渐近平坦末端的外在质量，A_S为分离指定末端与其他末端的最小紧致曲面面积下确界。论文不要求最外层自由边界极小曲面存在，也不对其他末端施加渐近或可积性条件。等号情形被完全刻画：当A_S=0时等号恰在欧氏半平面达到；完全悬链面以A_S>0实现等号；反之，若A_S>0时等号成立，则A_S由某个朝向指定末端是最外层的平坦自由边界盘取得，且S的对应分量是半悬链面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立平均凸平面曲面（如悬链面）上外在质量与分离面积之间的几何不等式，类似于广义相对论中质量-面积关系的刚性刻画，但去除了对最外层自由边界极小曲面存在性的假设，使结果适用于更广泛的曲面类，并得到完整的等号刚性分类。

Method: 论文采用几个几何分析方法：赋予渐近平坦末端可积的平均曲率以获得外在质量m_+(S)；定义A_S为分离指定末端与其他末端的紧致曲面面积的下确界；通过比较几何/变分技巧，在没有自由边界极小曲面存在假设的情形下建立不等式，并在等号情形下进行细致的刚性分析，包括A_S由平坦自由边界盘取得以及对应分量是半悬链面的结论。

Result: 建立了不等式 m_+(S) ≥ √(A_S/π)。等号情形完全分类：A_S=0时等号仅在欧氏半平面达到；完全悬链面给出A_S>0的等号例子；反之，若A_S>0且等号成立，则A_S由朝向指定末端最外层的平坦自由边界盘取得，且S对应分量是半悬链面。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了无最外层自由边界极小曲面假设下的质量-面积不等式，并在A_S=0和A_S>0两种情形下均给出等号刚性完整刻画，将平面曲面上的几何质量与分离面积联系起来，与完全悬链面、半悬链面的分类相吻合。

Abstract: Let $S\subset\mathbb R^3$ be a properly embedded mean-convex planar surface with finitely many ends. Designate one end as asymptotically flat, assume that $H_S$ is integrable there, and denote its extrinsic mass by $m_+(S)$. Let $A_S$ be the infimum of the areas of compact surfaces separating the distinguished end from all the others. We prove \[ m_+(S)\geq\sqrt{\frac{A_S}π}. \] No outermost free-boundary minimal surface is assumed, and no asymptotic or integrability condition is imposed on the other ends. If $A_S=0$, equality holds precisely for the Euclidean half-space. The complete catenoid realizes equality with $A_S>0$. Conversely, if equality holds with $A_S>0$, then $A_S$ is attained by a flat free-boundary disk that is outermost toward the distinguished end, and the corresponding component of $S$ is a half-catenoid.

</details>


### [3] [Pinching cones for positive isotropic curvature in dimensions seven and eight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26598)
*Jae Ho Cho*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文构造了n=7和8维中Hamilton ODE dR/dt=Q(R)的横截不变收缩锥族，将Brendle（n≥12）和Chen（9≤n≤11）的收缩估计扩展到低维情况。通过修复Chen构造中在n=8时失效的两个关键步骤（第二锥族的估计中保留耦合项；将粘合问题简化为弱PIC曲率算子锥上的单一线性不等式），以及重新平衡参数并对n=7的失效步骤采用直接多项式正定性证明，最终得到正各向同性曲率（PIC）紧流形的拓扑分类及κ-非坍缩古代解的分类，从n≥12（或n=4）扩展到n=7和8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brendle对n≥12、Chen对9≤n≤11建立的正向收缩锥族估计（pinching estimate）在n=7和8维度尚未解决。由于Chen构造中的多个关键步骤在这些低维中显式失效，需要新的技术手段来填补这一空白，从而完成低维情况下带正各向同性曲率流形的拓扑分类。

Method: 论文方法包括：(1) 在n=8时修复Chen构造中失效的第二锥族估计，通过保留在维度一般论证中被舍弃的耦合项；(2) 将两个锥族的粘合问题简化为弱PIC曲率算子锥上的单一线性不等式，并通过显式的有限帧不等式组合加以证明；(3) 在n=7时重新平衡两个锥族的参数，对第一锥族采用直接多项式正定性证书替代失效的端点约化，对粘合证书中失效的求均族部分采用基于第一Bianchi恒等式的不等式。

Result: 该论文成功在n=7和8维度构建了横截不变收缩锥族，得到收缩估计，并将其与Brendle的维度无关论证结合，给出了n=7、8维正各向同性曲率紧流形且不含平凡不可压缩(n-1)维空间形式的拓扑分类；同时与Cho-Li及Brendle-Naff的曲率改进和分类结果结合，得到了非紧κ-非坍缩、一致PIC的Ricci流古代解的分类，将此前n=4或n≥12的结果扩展到n=7和8。

Conclusion: 本文成功将收缩估计延伸至n=7和8的低维情形，克服了Chen构造中的多个结构性失效。通过保留耦合项、简化为线性不等式和多项式正定性证书等新论证，不仅完成了这两个关键维度的拓扑分类结果，还揭示了低维情形需要与高维不同的技术处理，为PIC流形的完全分类（含n=5变、6）提供了方法基础。

Abstract: We construct families of transversally invariant pinching cones for the Hamilton ODE dR/dt = Q(R) in dimensions n=7,8, thereby extending the pinching estimate that Brendle established for n >= 12 and Chen for 9 <= n <= 11. Two of the steps in Chen's construction genuinely fail at n=8. The first, an estimate for the second cone family, is repaired by keeping a coupling that is discarded in the dimension-general argument. The second is the gluing of the two cone families, for which Chen's estimate fails at n=8 by a definite margin. We replace it by a new argument which reduces the gluing to a single linear inequality on the cone of weakly PIC curvature operators, and proves that inequality by an explicit finite combination of frame inequalities. At n=7 two further steps break down: the endpoint reduction behind the first cone family, and part of the averaging family behind our gluing certificate. After re-balancing the parameters of both cone families, we replace the former by direct polynomial positivity certificates and the latter by inequalities resting on the first Bianchi identity. The resulting pinching estimate, together with dimension-free arguments of Brendle, yields in dimensions seven and eight the topological classification of compact manifolds with positive isotropic curvature that contain no nontrivial incompressible (n-1)-dimensional space forms, extending a theorem of Brendle from n >= 12. Together with the curvature-improvement and classification results of Cho-Li and Brendle-Naff, it yields the classification of noncompact kappa-noncollapsed ancient solutions to the Ricci flow with uniformly PIC, extending a theorem of Cho-Li from n=4 or n >= 12.

</details>


### [4] [Uniqueness of positively curved ancient Ricci flows on surfaces with boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26619)
*Kyeongho Bang,Eric Chen,Wenkui Du*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了二维带边曲面上的正曲率古代Ricci流的存在性与唯一性（模去与时间无关的微分同胚），假设边界测地曲率为正常数且直径一致有界。该流具有旋转对称性，其向后极限为平坦圆盘，向前极限为半球奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古代Ricci流的分类问题在流形无边界情形已有丰富成果，但带边流形的古代Ricci流分类此前尚无先例。本文旨在填补这一空白，为带边情形下的古代Ricci流分类提供首个实例。

Method: 通过假设均匀有界直径和常数正边界测地曲率，利用几何分析工具（包括紧致性、正则性估计以及边界条件处理），证明解的存在唯一性（模去随时间无关的微分同胚），并进一步分析其对称性及前后极限行为。

Result: 证明了该古代Ricci流唯一存在（模去时间无关微分同胚），且具有旋转对称性；向后极限为平坦圆盘，向前极限为半球奇点。这是带边界古代Ricci流分类的首个结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了二维带边正曲率古代Ricci流的完整分类，揭示了其对称性及奇点形成机制，为后续更一般情形的带边古代Ricci流分类研究奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: We establish the existence and uniqueness modulo time-independent diffeomorphisms of the positively curved ancient Ricci flow $(M^2, \partial M^2, g(t))$ on a two-dimensional surface with boundary, assuming uniformly bounded diameter and constant positive boundary geodesic curvature. In particular, this ancient Ricci flow is rotationally symmetric, its backward limit is the flat disk, and its forward limit is a half-spherical singularity. To our knowledge, this result is the first instance of a classification result for ancient Ricci flows with boundary.

</details>


### [5] [Extrinsic upper bounds for the harmonic mean of eigenvalues in curved manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26723)
*Hang Chen,Yuna Gao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了截面曲率上界为δ的黎曼流形中，m维闭可定向子流形M上拉普拉斯算子前m个非零特征值的调和均值的外在上界，推广了先前关于第一个非零特征值的相关结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在几何分析中，子流形上拉普拉斯算子特征值的估计是核心问题之一。以往的研究主要针对第一个非零特征值的上界估计，而关于前m个非零特征值（特别是其调和均值）的外在上界结果较少。本文旨在将前人关于第一非零特征值的上界结果推广到前m个非零特征值的调和均值，从而更全面地刻画子流形上的谱信息。

Method: 本文在截面曲率以δ为上界的黎曼流形中，考虑m维闭可定向子流形M。利用调和均值这一工具，结合子流形的外在几何（如平均曲率、第二基本形式等）与谱理论，推导出前m个非零特征值调和均值的外在（extrinsic）上界估计。

Result: 得到了子流形M上拉普拉斯算子前m个非零特征值调和均值的外在统一上界表达式，该上界同时推广并包含了先前针对第一非零特征值的上界结果，作为m=1情形的特例。

Conclusion: 该结果将子流形上第一非零特征值的外在上界估计推广至前m个特征值的调和均值情形，深化了对子流形谱性质的理解，为后续研究更一般的高阶特征值估计提供了理论工具与基础。

Abstract: Let $M$ be an $m (\ge2)$-dimensional closed orientable submanifold in a Riemannian manifold of sectional curvature bounded above by $δ$.
  We obtain the extrinsic upper bounds of the harmonic mean of the first $m$ nonzero eigenvalues of the Laplacian on $M$, which extend the previous related results for the first nonzero eigenvalues.

</details>


### [6] [On bubbling of Fueter maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26803)
*Jacek Rzemieniecki*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了哈密顿扰动Fueter方程解序列的冒泡现象。在Walpuski紧性定理框架下，有界能量序列弱收敛到Fueter映射，能量损失由余维二可折集上的缺陷测度记录。假设极限映射无非可去奇点、冒泡轨迹包含非平凡Lipschitz弧且气泡沿此弧附着于极限映射，则证明极限哈密顿量属于无限余维的例外子集。证明结合了超凯勒流形中有理扫掠轨迹的定量控制与横截性论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解哈密顿扰动Fueter方程解序列的冒泡行为及其非典型性。作者期望揭示在无非可去奇点的条件下，冒泡现象的极端非一般性（nongeneric），从而深化对Fueter映射紧性与正则性结构的认识。

Method: 结合两个关键工具：(1) 超凯勒流形中有理扫掠（rational sweepout）轨迹的定量控制，用以约束冒泡轨迹的几何行为；(2) 横截性（transversality）论证，用以证明极限哈密顿量落入一个无限余维的例外集合。

Result: 在极限映射无非可去奇点、冒泡轨迹含非平凡Lipschitz弧且气泡沿该弧附着的假设下，证明极限哈密顿量属于无限余维的例外子集，从而强有力地表明哈密顿扰动Fueter映射的冒泡在无非可去奇点情形下是高度非典型的。

Conclusion: 冒泡现象在哈密顿扰动Fueter方程中（在无非可去奇点假设下）是高度非一般的。作者预期Lipschitz弧与附着假设均为自动成立，这将进一步强化上述结论，即冒泡的非典型性是本质性的而非技术性的人为条件。

Abstract: We study bubbling for sequences of solutions of the Hamiltonian-perturbed Fueter equation. By Walpuski's compactness theorem, a bounded-energy sequence converges weakly to a Fueter map, with energy loss recorded by a defect measure supported on a codimension-two rectifiable set. Assuming that the limiting map has no non-removable singularities, the bubbling locus contains a nontrivial Lipschitz arc and the bubbles attach to the limiting map along this arc, we show that the limiting Hamiltonian lies in an exceptional subset of infinite codimension. The proof combines quantitative control of rational sweepout loci in hyperkähler manifolds with a transversality argument. We expect both the Lipschitz-arc and attachment assumptions to be automatic. This strongly suggests that, in the absence of non-removable singularities, bubbling of Hamiltonian-perturbed Fueter maps is highly nongeneric.

</details>


### [7] [On the existence of maximal foliations in general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26816)
*Bing-Long Chen,Ruo-Bin Wu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在真空爱因斯坦约束方程下，证明了任意满足平均曲率小性条件的渐近平坦柯西初值曲面附近都存在最大叶层结构（maximal foliation）的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大叶层结构作为爱因斯坦方程求解中的强大规范条件，在闵可夫斯基时空全局稳定性和有界L^2曲率猜想中发挥了关键作用。然而，当初始超曲面不满足最大条件时，传统的椭圆方法在构造最大叶层结构时会遇到根本性困难。因此，需要发展新的方法来克服这一障碍。

Method: 论文采用了一种新的构造方法，在初值满足真空爱因斯坦约束方程且平均曲率满足适当小性条件的前提下，于任意渐近平坦柯西曲面附近构造最大叶层结构。该方法绕过了传统椭圆方法在非最大初始超曲面上遇到的困难。

Result: 成功证明了在平均曲率小性条件下，任意渐近平坦柯西初值曲面附近都存在最大叶层结构的构造。

Conclusion: 该工作将最大叶层结构的适用范围从最大初始超曲面推广到了平均曲率较小的更一般渐近平坦初值，为爱因斯坦方程的求解提供了更广泛的规范选择。

Abstract: It is well known that the Einstein equations are tensor equations for a Lorentzian metric. Hence, choosing a suitable gauge condition is crucial for solving them. As a powerful gauge condition, maximal foliations have played a pivotal role in two groundbreaking works in general relativity: the global stability of Minkowski spacetime and the bounded L^2 curvature conjecture. Nevertheless, if the initial hypersurface fails to be maximal, the usual elliptic approach for constructing maximal foliations encounters fundamental difficulties when solving the Einstein equations.
  The purpose of the paper is to provide a construction of maximal foliations around any initial asymptotically flat Cauchy surface satisfying vacuum Einstein constraint equations, under a suitable smallness condition on the mean curvature.

</details>


### [8] [Evolutes, involutes and parallels of Legendre curves in hyperbolic and de Sitter 2-spaces via rotational transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26823)
*Nozomi Nakatsuyama,Masatomo Takahashi,Anjie Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的方法，通过旋转变换（旋转、boost和零旋转）来构造Legendre曲线的渐屈线、渐伸线和平行曲线的移动标架，并系统给出了双曲和de Sitter 2-空间中这些曲线的定义，同时讨论了它们之间的联系与对偶性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在微分几何中，渐屈线、渐伸线和平行曲线是经典曲线理论中的重要概念，但当曲线位于双曲空间和de Sitter空间等非欧几何背景时，缺乏统一的构造框架。现有方法在处理这类曲线时不够系统，需要一种统一的方法来构建移动标架并理清各曲线之间的几何关系。

Method: 本文采用统一的方法论，利用三种旋转变换——旋转（rotation）、boost变换和零旋转（null rotation）——来构造Legendre曲线的渐屈线、渐伸线和平行曲线的移动标架。具体地，作者系统地定义了双曲和de Sitter 2-空间中Legendre曲线的渐屈线、渐伸线和平行曲线，并在此基础上讨论这些曲线之间的内在联系与对偶关系。

Result: 成功建立了统一的移动标架构造框架，系统给出了双曲和de Sitter 2-空间中Legendre曲线的渐屈线、渐伸线和平行曲线的定义，并揭示了这些曲线之间存在的联系与对偶性质。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究非欧几何空间（双曲空间和de Sitter空间）中Legendre曲线的相关曲线提供了统一的理论框架，深化了对这些经典曲线概念在不同几何背景下的理解。

Abstract: We develop a unified approach to constructing the moving frames of the evolute, involute and parallel of a Legendre curve via rotational transformations (rotation, boost and null rotation). The definitions of the evolute, involute and parallel of a Legendre curve in hyperbolic and de Sitter 2-spaces are systematically presented. Furthermore, the connections and dualities among these curves are discussed.

</details>


### [9] [Finite semisimplicial sets, differential graded algebras, and cellular sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26953)
*Jan-Willem Van Looy,Ferdinando Zanchetta*

Main category: math.DG

Result: 主要结果包括：Ω^•_S构造的实质像被完全刻画并得到范畴等价（一维退化为图-FODC反等价）；S上细胞层范畴与Ω^•_S-模范畴之间建立等价；联络由边线性映射刻画；2-单纯形上曲率等于直接边传输与复合边传输之差；当边传输可逆时，所有2-单纯形上曲率消失等价于边传输可胶合延拓为P_S上的联络层。

Abstract: We develop a differential graded model for finite non-singular semisimplicial sets and their cellular sheaves. To a finite non-singular semisimplicial set \(S\), we associate an exterior DGA \(Ω^\bullet_S\), extending the classical anti-equivalence between finite simple directed graphs and first-order differential calculi to higher degrees. We characterize the essential image of this construction, obtaining an equivalence of categories that recovers the graph-FODC correspondence in dimension one. For a fixed \(S\), we then characterize a category of differential graded \(Ω^\bullet_S\)-modules equivalent to the category of cellular sheaves on $S$, thereby giving a differential refinement of the usual incidence-algebra description. Finally, we study connections and curvature in this framework. Connections are described by edgewise linear maps and their curvature on a 2-dimensional simplex is the difference between direct and composite edge transport. When the edge transports are invertible, vanishing of this curvature on every \(2\)-simplex can equivalently be seen as a gluing criterion to extend the given edge transports to a connection sheaf on \(P_S\).

Abstract: It is not a priori clear which of the Gauduchon connections is better suited to LCK and Vaisman manifolds. Our results single out the Bismut connection as the privileged one for the non-Kähler lcK and Vaisman geometry. Abstract: We show that the spacelike solutions to the curvature flow for curves on the De Sitter space are in correspondence with the solutions to the inverse curvature flow on the 2-dimensional hyperbolic space, that on the De Sitter space, the timelike solutions to the curvature flow are in correspondence with the timelike solutions to the inverse curvature flow, and that the solutions curve shortening flow on the 2-dimensional hyperbolic space are in correspondence with the solutions to the spacelike solutions to the inverse curvature flow on the De Sitter space. We observe that a spacelike or timelike curve on the De Sitter space is a soliton solution to the curvature flow (resp. inverse curvature flow) if and only if its curvature (resp. inverse of its curvature) can be written as the inner product between its tangent vector field and a fixed vector $v$ of the 3-dimensional Minkowski space. We prove that for each vector $v$, there exists a 2-parameter family of timelike (spacelike) soliton solutions to the curvature flow and to the inverse curvature flow on the De Sitter space. We show that there exists no non-trivial complete timelike soliton. There exist non-trivial complete spacelike solutions. As a consequence of curvature flow, we obtain the behavior of the soliton solutions to the inverse curvature flow on the De Sitter space and hyperbolic space.

Thus this intrinsic distance-weighted inequality characterizes exactly the simultaneous strictness of the stability and minimizing properties. Second, without either strictness assumption, every regular area-minimizing hypercone satisfies the scale-invariant quadratic inequality \[
  \frac{\operatorname{Per}(F;B_R)-\operatorname{Per}(E;B_R)}{R^n}
  \geq
  c_\mathbf{C}
  \left(
  \frac{|F\mathbin\triangle E|}{R^{n+1}}
  \right)^2. \] This extends the inequality previously established for area-minimizing Lawson cones. Finally, the area-minimizing assumption is unnecessary for our spectral result: for every stable regular minimal hypercone, the first Dirichlet eigenvalue $λ_\mathbf{C}^D(R)$ of the Jacobi operator on $\mathbf{C}\cap B_R$ is given exactly by \[

  =
  \frac{j_{b_1,1}^2}{R^2},
  \qquad
  b_1^2
  =
  \frac{(n-2)^2}{4}+μ_1, \] where $μ_1$ is the first eigenvalue of the link Jacobi operator and $j_{b_1,1}$ is the first positive zero of the Bessel function $J_{b_1}$. In particular, this identifies the optimal Dirichlet spectral constant for every stable regular minimal hypercone.

</details>

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Let X be a smooth, complex Fano variety, and delta(X) its Lefschetz defect. It is known that if delta(X) is at least 4, then X is isomorphic to a product SxT where S is a surface, whereas if delta(X)=3, X admits a del Pezzo fibration with a prescribed structure. (ii) An upper bound on the dimension of the centroid that is essentially attained by our examples. (iii) The development of a geometric technique to write down border rank decomposition of tensors using centroids and "extended centroids". (iv) The technique is applied to tensors of this paper to prove they are of minimal border rank. The technique is versatile and enables us to geometrically derive and improve upon previous ad hoc decompositions. (v) The construction of symmetric tensors with large centroids and proof that they are wild in the sense of Buczyńska-Buczyński.

The geometric technique also constructs new "better" tensors for Strassen's laser method from old, and we apply this to the tensors of Strassen and Schönhage to get better tensors in the sense that they give better upper bounds on the exponent than the original tensors.

Abstract: We present results of a systematic validation of the \lalsuite{} timing model for continuous gravitational waves against \pint{}, a modern high-accuracy pulsar-timing package. An accurate timing model is essential for tracking the signal phase, and hence for detecting and accurately characterizing continuous gravitational waves. In order to quantify the impact of timing inaccuracies, we derive and validate the leading-order relation $μ\approx (2πf)^2\stddtau^2$, where $μ$ is the fractional loss of signal power, $f$ is the signal frequency, and $\stddtau^2$ is the variance of the timing errors. We then compare the solar-system and binary components of the \lalsuite{} timing model against the corresponding models in \pint{}. With the original \lalsuite{} Einstein-delay implementation, the total disagreement is dominated by that component and has $\stddtau\simeq\SI{2.3}{\micro\second}$ (corresponding to $μ\simeq\SI{0.02}{\percent}$ at $f=\SI{1000}{\hertz}$). With the newer Einstein-delay implementation, the total disagreement (over one year) drops to $\stddtau\lesssim\SI{31}{\nano\second}$ (or $μ\lesssim\num{4e-8}$ at $f=\SI{1000}{\hertz}$) and is dominated by the observatory contribution to the \Romer{} delay, owing to the approximate Earth-rotation model used by \lalsuite{}. We additionally test binary delays using orbital parameters from \num{474} catalogued binary pulsars and verify the self-consistency of the \lalsuite{} source-time derivatives. Finally, we derive and discuss the \lalsuite{} Shapiro delay for signals passing through the solar interior, a case only relevant to gravitational waves.

Abstract: We investigate static spherically symmetric vacuum solutions in higher-dimensional $f(R)$ gravity, beginning with the five-dimensional Starobinsky model governed by the action $f(R) = R + αR^2 - 2Λ$. Finally, we demonstrate that expanding the gravitational action to a multi-term polynomial hierarchy circumvents this strict limitation. By including curvature corrections up to $\mathcal{O}(R^3)$, the extended geometric degrees of freedom simultaneously satisfy the trace constraint and the stability criterion. Furthermore, we establish the exact parameter space boundaries that ensure not only asymptotic vacuum stability but strict global stability ($f'(R) > 0$ for all $R$), allowing for the dynamical generation of exact, globally ghost-free vacuum spacetimes in $D \ge 5$ purely from higher-order geometric terms. Abstract: We study pair creation for quantum fields of spin $0$, $1/2$, and $1$ in the presence of weak gravitational and gauge backgrounds. Using resummed heat-kernel techniques, we obtain closed expressions for the pair creation probability up to quadratic order in the ``generalized curvatures''. In addition, for gravitational backgrounds, we recast the pair production probability in terms of the matter distribution required for consistency with the Einstein equation. Finally, to illustrate the powerfulness of our methods, we analyze the effect of pair creation in a binary star system.

Abstract: We investigate the unitarity of quantum evolution relative to an internal time defined by a local observer's clock. The observer is modeled as a relativistic particle carrying both a clock and a matter-field detector, analyzed first on a fixed curved background and subsequently within a fully diffeomorphism-invariant theory of dynamical gravity. In the former case, we find that evolution with respect to the internal clock time is generally nonunitary, implying a violation of the Einstein equivalence principle at the quantum level. In contrast, in the latter case, diffeomorphism invariance allows us to adopt observer-centric coordinates without loss of generality. On the resulting partially-reduced phase space, one of the diffeomorphism generators becomes linear in the clock Hamiltonian, generating a relational evolution that is consistent with the remaining diffeomorphism constraints. Assuming that an effective quantum field theory exists to be consistent with the diffeomorphism invariance, these features ensure unitary evolution relative to the internal clock, thereby preserving the equivalence principle even in the quantum regime. Our results highlight the fundamental role of diffeomorphism invariance in shaping relational unitary evolution from the perspective of a local observer.

Abstract: Weak bialgebras are natural generalizations of bialgebras that play a fundamental role in quantum groups, tensor categories, and representation theory. In this paper, we systematically classify all (weak) bialgebra structures on the finite-dimensional semisimple algebra \(\Bbbk^{\oplus n}\), with a distinguished basis of primitive orthogonal idempotents. For the bialgebra case, we prove that such structures are in bijection with finite monoids, and that bialgebra isomorphisms correspond exactly to monoid isomorphisms. Extending the result to weak bialgebras, we show that every weak bialgebra structure uniquely determines a finite small category.

Abstract: We consider the motion of a charged test particle confined to the round unit sphere in a uniform ambient magnetic field. We use an extension of Noether's theorem for systems with magnetic forces to reduce the problem to a quadrature. The rotation number of a trajectory, the azimuth it gains over one oscillation in latitude, is a complete elliptic integral of the third kind in Legendre form. A trajectory closes if and only if that advance is a rational multiple of a full turn. The criterion holds on every level set of the two first integrals. Then, we differentiate the rotation number with respect to the half-cyclotron frequency and sign that derivative on each of the two branches into which a level set divides. The rotation number sees the charge, the mass, the field and the speed through a single dimensionless ratio, that of the half-cyclotron frequency to the speed. A closed trajectory therefore fixes a value of that ratio. We say how many values carry a given rational winding. The poles are attainable on a single level set, where the motion reduces to a pendulum. The field has no flux through the sphere, so it is globally exact. We compute the Mañé strict critical value of the resulting exact magnetic flow. Above that value the closed trajectories are closed Reeb orbits of an explicit contact form.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce and study (fermionic and bosonic) invertible quasi-local algebras over uniformly locally finite metric spaces $X$ with infinite-dimensional local von Neumann algebras. We show that the group of Brauer equivalence classes of such algebras is isomorphic to both the group of phases of invertible states and the group of stable equivalence classes of quantum cellular automata over $X\times \mathbb{Z}$. Using K-theory of the symmetric monoidal category of invertible quasi-local algebras and bounded spread isomorphisms, we propose a definition of an $Ω$-spectrum of invertible phases as conjectured by Kitaev. We then show that the $c=\frac{1}{2}$ chiral Majorana fermion net and the $(E_{8})_{1}$ conformal net provide Brauer non-trivial invertible quasi-local algebras, thus providing explicit constructions of non-trivial invertible states and quantum cellular automata on $\mathbb{Z}^{2}$. In addition, we show that the time-slice nets of rational diagonal conformal field theories admit lattice degrees of freedom, which implies the discretization of any holomorphic conformal net is invertible.

  For $n=1,2$, we prove that the functor $\mathrm{Mod}_n:\mathrm{Mrt}_{E_n}(\mathcal{C})\to \mathrm{Mrt}_{E_{n-1}}(\mathrm{LMod}^{\mathrm{rep}}(\mathcal{C}))$ is an equivalence, relating the algebraic description of higher Morita theory to its realization in terms of module categories. In this formulation, the notion of a bi-bimodule arises naturally and provides a common framework for local and confined modules. Its explicit orientation data, together with the corresponding fusion rules, clarifies the relations among defects arising from condensation in topological orders.

Abstract: Counting processes provide a fundamental description of stochastic events ranging from photon detection to clock ticks. A central question is how accurately such events can be timed when only finite memory resources are available. Here, we investigate this problem within a general framework of finite-dimensional classical and quantum counting processes. We derive a rigorous finite-memory variance bound obeyed by every classical $d$-state counting process, which is tight and saturated by a discrete Erlang-type ladder process. Through numerical optimization, we identify quantum counting processes that violate this classical bound, achieving smaller first-tick fluctuations than any classical process with the same memory size and mean tick time. For the qubit case, we further derive an analytical large-mean bound within a single-Kraus no-tick family, showing that the quantum advantage persists asymptotically within this class. The optimized quantum processes exhibit coherent conditioned dynamics and approach a continuous-time quantum-jump description as the mean increases. Our results establish a finite-memory quantum advantage in temporal precision and connect discrete-time counting processes with continuous-time quantum timekeeping.

</details>

Abstract: We prove that $(1-o(1))$-fidelity $N$-qubit Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) encoding can be performed in time $O(\log N/N)$ using all-to-all 2-local Hamiltonians with bounded 2-qubit interaction terms. This saturates the theoretical lower bound $Ω(\log N/N)$, and by rescaling, also saturates the lower bound on signaling time for Hamiltonians with power-law interaction strengths $1/r^γ$, $γ<d$ in dimension $d$. The protocol uses spin-squeezing dynamics combined with quantum signal processing.

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子反常（特别是Lieb-Schultz-Mattis定理）如何约束开放量子系统中的动力学特征和时间关联。通过时空对偶性，作者将含有混合反常对称性的d维重复量子通道的刘维尔算符映射为(d+1)维混合态对称性保护拓扑（mSPT）相，并引入扭曲Renyi-N关联函数（TRNC）作为时间关联的探针，证明了其与mSPT奇怪关联函数的对偶关系。此外，作者提出刘维尔奇异谱比刘维尔谱本身更能作为量子反常的诊断工具，并展示了其与mSPT的算子纠缠谱的对偶性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子反常（如LSM定理）在传统平衡态多体系统中已被充分研究，但其在开放量子系统动力学中的影响尚未被系统探索。作者希望理解LSM约束如何在开放量子系统的时间关联和动力学特征中体现，从而为开放量子系统中的反常现象提供新的理论框架和诊断工具。

Method: 作者采用时空对偶性方法：将d维重复量子通道的刘维尔算符映射到(d+1)维混合态对称性保护拓扑（mSPT）相。初始态和稳态被识别为边界态。引入扭曲Renyi-N关联函数（TRNC）作为时间关联探针，并证明其与mSPT奇怪关联函数对偶。最后提出刘维尔奇异谱作为反常诊断，并将其与mSPT算子纠缠谱建立对偶关系。

Abstract: Quantum anomalies strongly constrain the possible behavior of many-body systems. A prime example is the Lieb-Schultz-Mattis (LSM) theorem, which relates UV symmetry and filling constraints to IR features of the energy spectrum and ground-state structure. Here, we ask how LSM constraints shape dynamical signatures and temporal correlations in open quantum systems. Using a spacetime duality, we show that the Liouvillian of a $d$-dimensional repeated quantum channel with a mixed anomaly between strong $S$ and weak $G$ symmetry can be mapped to a $(d{+}1)$-dimensional mixed-state symmetry-protected topological (mSPT) phase. Under this correspondence, the initial and steady states of the channel are identified with boundary states of the higher-dimensional mSPT in the presence of bulk projection. We further introduce the twisted Renyi-$N$ correlator (TRNC) as a probe of temporal correlations in the channel and demonstrate that it is dual to the mSPT strange correlator, providing a direct bulk-boundary route to diagnose long-range temporal order implied by the LSM anomaly. Abstract: We develop a nonperturbative, group-theoretic quantization of the effective guiding center theory of a charged particle drifting in a magnetic field in two spatial dimensions, and compare the resulting quantum theory with a corresponding coarse graining of the underlying microscopic theory. The reduced phase space is the physical space itself, so quantization leads to noncommuting spatial coordinates, and the effective theory loses access to the metric structure of physical space, retaining only its area structure. By formulating a prescription to match between the microscopic and effective quantum theories, we find that the predictions of the quantized effective theory are generally consistent with those of the microscopic theory. Abstract: Fast and reliable qubit reset is essential for the efficient operation of quantum processors. Abstract: Accurately estimating expectation values of observables from a finite number of measurement shots is a central challenge in quantum information science. Informationally overcomplete measurements provide a route to reduce estimation variance through optimized classical post-processing. However, the interplay between measurement geometry and dual-frame construction remains largely unexplored. In this work, we study Platonic solid POVMs ---highly symmetric, overcomplete single-qubit measurements whose effects correspond to the vertices of the five Platonic solids on the Bloch sphere--- for the estimation of molecular Hamiltonians. We demonstrate that optimized Platonic solid POVMs can outperform standard randomized Pauli measurements, provided the MPS bond dimension is sufficient to faithfully represent the target state. Abstract: Quantum computers require fast and accurate methods for qubit state detection. Here, we demonstrate a superconducting phase-transition radio-frequency single-electron transistor (PTSET), a charge sensor for semiconductor spin qubits whose response is enhanced by a superconducting-to-normal phase transition. The transition is engineered by linking the sensor current to a low-critical-current, high-kinetic-inductance inductor integrated into the radio-frequency matching network.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交唯我论常因否认观测事件的绝对性而遭到误解，批评者往往在经典、非视角性语言中提出反对意见，但这些反对意见实际上偷偷带回了社交唯我论所拒绝的假设。本文旨在重构这些误解，并表明它们有助于揭示任何认真对待非绝对事实的解释所需的概念纪律。

Result: 社交唯我论并非退化为心理学唯我论，而是一种视角话语的纪律。当观测事实被适当地索引到视角时，表面矛盾得以消解，关于意识、交流和他心的反对意见也失去效力。视角逻辑与经典逻辑的关系在结构上类同于量子概率演算与经典（Kolmogorovian）概率论的关系。

Conclusion: 社交唯我论的核心在于：事实必须被理解为视角性的、索引化的，而非属于单一绝对的事实域。对它的常见反对源于将视角索引删除后误当作全局陈述来处理。正确理解和运用视角索引后，社交唯我论是一种自洽且严谨的量子力学解释框架，其逻辑结构在数学上与经典逻辑的结构性对比类似量子概率与经典概率的对比。

Abstract: Convivial Solipsism is easily misunderstood. Because it denies the absoluteness of observed events while preserving the universality of quantum mechanics, it invites objections that are often formulated in a classical, non-perspectival language. Such objections appear powerful only because they smuggle back into the discussion the very assumptions that Convivial Solipsism rejects. This article reconstructs a family of misunderstandings that arise in discussions of Convivial Solipsism, especially in connection with Wigner's friend and extended Wigner's friend scenarios. The aim is not merely defensive. The objections are useful because they identify the conceptual discipline required by any interpretation that takes non-absolute facts seriously. The central claim is that observational facts must be indexed to perspectives. A statement such as "the friend saw outcome A" is not a complete factual statement within Convivial Solipsism. It must be reformulated as "from the friend's perspective, the friend saw outcome A" or "from Wigner's perspective, the friend reported outcome B." The mistake consists in removing these indices and then treating the resulting expressions as if they belonged to a single global factual domain. This leads to apparent contradictions and to false dilemmas concerning consciousness, communication, other minds, and intersubjectivity. When that is taken into account, it appears that Convivial Solipsism is not a retreat into psychological solipsism, but a discipline of perspectival discourse. It is also shown that perspectival logic stands to classical logic as the quantum calculus of probability stands to Kolmogorovian probability theory.

Abstract: Understanding the temporal and spatial dependence of temperature is critical in high-performance cryogenic devices. Typical thermometry techniques struggle to simultaneously combine high bandwidth, sensitivity and on-chip integration, limiting their ability to measure fast intra-device thermal fluctuations. Here, we demonstrate a time-resolved thermometry platform based on TiN kinetic inductance resonators integrated in a 22 nm FDSOI chip. By tracking temperature-dependent shifts in the resonant frequency, we achieve sub-millikelvin temperature sensitivity down to temperatures of 100 mK. Time-resolved on-chip pulsed heating experiments as a function of distance reveal an onset delay, consistent with a quasi-ballistic heat propagation velocity of 3.9 ${\pm}$ 0.1 mm $μ$s${}^{-1}$. We also show that elevated temperatures increase net thermal conductance, shortening thermal relaxation times across all spatial separations. This behaviour manifests in two distinct regimes: a substrate-limited regime at 100 mK, where cooling rates vary with heater distance, and a Kapitza boundary-limited regime at 400 mK, where thermal relaxation becomes more spatially uniform.These measurements demonstrate kinetic inductance thermometry's ability to rapidly probe non-equilibrium temperature dynamics in cryogenic devices such as quantum processors.

Abstract: Can CHSH nonlocality survive particle loss without applying an explicit recovery operation? In principle, any deterministic recovery can be absorbed into the measurement. Operationally, we show that the answer depends on the allowed measurements on the lossy system. We distinguish flagged erasure, in which each lost particle leaves a detectable record, from unflagged deletion, in which no such record remains. For flagged erasure and survival probability $η>1/2$, using known quantum-capacity results, we show measurements that asymptotically approach the quantum maximum $2\sqrt2$. In contrast, for $η\le1/2$, CHSH violation is impossible for both loss models. Then, we construct explicit recovery-free protocols using permutation-invariant encodings built from $n$-qubit Dicke states $| D_N^n\rangle$ and measurements on the surviving particles. A one-excitation $(N=1)$ encoding violates CHSH for $η>1/\sqrt{2}$. Increasing the excitation number $N$ yields a family of protocols that violates CHSH for $η>η_G=(\sqrt{5}-1)/2$, with the asymptotic golden ratio approached as $N \to \infty$. Finally, we present a sparse-deletion binomial PI protocol that guarantees CHSH violation for up to $O(\sqrt n)$ deletion errors. Our results distinguish fundamental limits imposed by loss from those set by explicit measurements without recovery.

</details>

Conclusion: 可编程的腔相互作用为分布式信号的纠缠定制提供了可扩展的途径，建立了利用量子关联增强分布式传感的可行框架。

Abstract: Field sensing with state-of-the-art atom interferometers is restricted to the use of uncorrelated devices op- erating in parallel. We can overcome this limitation by using distributed sensing protocols where quantum correlations among spatially-separated devices are engineered in the spatial mode carrying the signal. We show that a tweezer array in a cavity offers an ideal testbed to engineer quantum states for distributed sensing, with the possibility to generate entanglement both within and between the clouds. The competition between local and intercloud cavity-mediated exchange allows the sign and spatial pattern of the intercloud couplings to select the squeezed mode. For two ensembles, positive coupling produces uniform collective squeezing, whereas negative coupling generates strong staggered, nonlocal squeezing. A semiclassical analysis reveals a counter-twisting- like phase-space flow, qualitatively distinct from standard one-axis twisting. The analysis and results can be further generalized to a larger number of ensembles. We apply the scheme to differential Ramsey interferometry with common phase noise spanning the full 2 πrange, the resulting staggered states reduce the phase uncertainty below the standard quantum limit, with an ellipse estimator approaching the Cramèr-Rao bound. These results establish programmable cavity interactions as a scalable route to entanglement tailored to distributed signals.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者系统考察了先前工作中提出的干涉仪几何结构，重点审视了对粒子轨迹竖直段相位贡献的常用忽略近似；随后推导了包含完整粒子轨迹的精确解析解，并对不同参数区间下竖直臂对累积相位的影响进行了详细分析。

Result: 分析表明忽略竖直段相位的近似可能导致错误预测，在某些参数区间会产生本应存在纠缠却得到零结果的假阴性结论。精确解显示竖直臂可以显著影响累积相位，并且发现了在特定构型下引力诱导纠缠完全消失的情况，这是简化处理无法捕捉的。

Abstract: The pursuit of a quantum theory of gravity, aiming to unify general relativity and quantum mechanics, remains one of the most enduring challenges in physics. Because of the extreme energy scales associated with the Planck regime, direct experimental evidence for quantum gravity remains elusive. However, recent proposals suggest that quantum entanglement between two massive particles may provide a pathway to probe the quantum nature of gravity. In this study, we examine the interferometer geometries proposed in these works, with particular attention to the commonly used approximation that neglects phase contributions from the vertical segments of the particle trajectories. Our analysis shows that this approximation can lead to incorrect predictions and, in certain parameter regimes, to null results where entanglement would otherwise be expected. We derive exact solutions that incorporate the full particle trajectories and demonstrate that the vertical arms can significantly affect the accumulated phase. Crucially, we identify configurations in which the induced entanglement vanishes entirely, a feature missed by simplified treatments. These findings show that accounting for the full interferometer geometry is not merely a refinement, but is essential for accurately assessing gravity-induced entanglement.

### [98] [Emergent non-Markovianity in time-delayed waveguide QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26427)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统不可避免地与环境耦合，马尔可夫近似是一种常用简化方法，但其假设环境在系统演化时间尺度上"无记忆"。在相互作用的多体系统中，很难判断多个集体时间尺度中哪些是相关的，因此需要研究马尔可夫近似的适用边界，尤其是对可扩展量子器件的实际意义。

Method: 采用时间延迟的波导QED设置，考虑单激发子（single excitation）情形。通过固定最大传播时间并逐步增加发射体数量，系统性地分析非马尔可夫性的出现条件。具体对比超辐射态和亚辐射态，分别考察系统时间尺度与浴时间尺度之间的竞争关系。

Result: 即使延迟时间很小（小延迟极限），当发射体数量足够多时，集体效应仍能导致非马尔可夫性。记忆效应的影响具有状态依赖性：超辐射态中相关时间尺度是超辐射寿命与端到端延迟时间的对比；而亚辐射态的两个时间尺度均依赖于具体态的结构，更加复杂。

Conclusion: 该工作表明在多体量子系统中必须谨慎进行马尔可夫近似，并指出了在将量子设备扩展到大规模系统时可能遇到的潜在陷阱。

Abstract: Quantum systems are invariably coupled to a surrounding environment. A common theoretical method to simplify the problem is to trace over the environment under a Markov approximation, which assumes that the environment holds no memory over the timescales that the system evolves under. In interacting many-body systems, it is often not trivial to extract which of the multiple collective timescales are relevant. We consider a time-delayed waveguide QED setup with a single excitation. By fixing the maximum propagation time and increasing the number of emitters, we show that, even for small delay times, collective effects are sufficient to cause non-Markovianity. The impact of memory is intrinsically state-dependent. For superradiant states, the relevant system timescale is the superradiant lifetime while the relevant bath timescale is the end-to-end delay time. For subradiant states, both timescales depend on the structure of the specific state. Our results demonstrate the importance of prudently making Markov approximations in quantum many-body systems, and highlight potential pitfalls to avoid in scaling up quantum devices to large system sizes.

</details>


### [99] [Stochastic transport of a Goldstone mode in a self-organized atomic crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27387)
*Zhanhai Yu,Di Xiang,Xiaotian Zhang,Hao Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在光学环形腔内的自组织原子晶体中，首次直接跟踪了Goldstone模式的随机输运过程，发现其扩散常数随1/N标度，表明所有原子作为刚体整体运动，并通过调节Langevin驱动力和腔介导阻尼，使归一化扩散常数坍缩到单一普适曲线上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续对称性自发破缺产生的无质量Goldstone模式在激发谱、模式软化或集体振荡中已被广泛研究，但其在固有涨落和耗散下的时域输运行为尚未被探索，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用光学环形腔将序参量相位映射到晶体在实空间的位置，在不施加外部扰动的情况下，通过基本光子散射反冲驱动集体输运，同时利用腔耗散产生摩擦；通过测量腔输出相位来监测原子及其自生成光晶格的单条轨迹，并调节Langevin驱动力与腔介导阻尼进行系统性研究。

Result: 发现扩散常数随1/N减小，表明所有原子作为刚性客体集体运动而非独立扩散；通过调节Langevin驱动力和腔介导阻尼，归一化扩散常数坍缩到单一普适曲线。

Conclusion: 该工作将连续对称性破缺的研究从激发频率测量扩展到实时输运跟踪，为非平衡集体输运、声子动力学以及驱动-耗散量子物质中缺陷形成的研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Spontaneous breaking of a continuous symmetry produces a massless Goldstone mode that can evolve across a degenerate manifold at zero energy cost. Goldstone modes have been identified primarily through excitation spectra, mode softening or collective oscillations. However, their time-domain transport under intrinsic fluctuations and dissipation has remained largely unexplored. Here we directly track the stochastic transport of a Goldstone mode in a self-organized atomic crystal inside an optical ring cavity. The ring cavity maps the order-parameter phase onto the real-space position of the emergent crystal. Without any external perturbation, fundamental photon-scattering recoil drives the collective transport, while cavity dissipation generates friction. We monitor individual trajectories of the atoms and their self-generated optical lattice by measuring the cavity output phase. We find that the diffusion constant decreases as $1/N$, indicating that all atoms move collectively as a rigid object rather than independently. By tuning the Langevin driving force and cavity-mediated damping, we show that the normalized diffusion constant collapses onto a single universal curve. This work extends the study of continuous symmetry breaking from excitation-frequency measurements to real-time tracking of transport, and opens routes for studying non-equilibrium collective transport, phonon dynamics, and defect formation in driven-dissipative quantum matter.

</details>


### [100] [Detuning-robust Rydberg entangling gates from echoed pulses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26446)
*Zhubing Jia,Yichao Yu,Xiye Hu,Lintao Li,Jacob Zheng,Christopher Monroe,Jacob P. Covey*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的Rydberg双量子比特门脉冲设计方案，通过将所有的领头阶失谐误差转嫁为单量子比特Z旋转并用回波序列消除，实现了对单个原子上任意失谐的一阶不敏感的ZZ门，将保真度保持在10^-3以下，并显著降低了对激光频率稳定性和强度均匀性的要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子阵列中基于Rydberg的双量子比特门保真度主要受限于激光强度不均匀性和失谐误差（频率校准错误、背景场漂移、中间态光移和多普勒频移）。量子最优控制可以抑制前者，但没有任何脉冲能使控制-Z门对失谐一阶不敏感，这是一个根本性的障碍。

Method: 作者通过重新设计Rydberg门脉冲，使得所有领头阶失谐误差都被转化为单量子比特Z旋转，再利用回波（echoed）序列将这些Z旋转消除。由此构造的ZZ门对任意单原子失谐都具有一阶不敏感性。

Result: 得到的最大纠缠ZZ门对两个原子上的任意失谐均无领头阶响应，在比以往门设计方案更宽的单原子失谐范围内保持保真度高于10^-3（即infidelity低于10^-3）。此外，发现残余误差的大部分来自计算子空间之外的布居，这些可以被转化为可宣告的擦除（erasure）。

Conclusion: 该工作显著降低了对激光频率稳定性、强度均匀性、场校准和原子温度的要求，为使用中性原子实现实用的容错量子计算铺平了道路。

Abstract: Rydberg-based two-qubit gate fidelities in neutral atom arrays are limited chiefly by laser intensity inhomogeneity and by detuning errors from frequency miscalibration, background-field drift, intermediate state light shift and Doppler shifts. Quantum optimal control can suppress the former, but no pulse can render a controlled-Z gate first-order insensitive to detuning. Here we show that this obstruction can be circumvented by designing Rydberg gate pulses with every leading-order detuning error relegated to single-qubit Z-rotations, which an echoed sequence removes. The resulting maximally-entangling ZZ gate has no leading-order response to arbitrary detunings on either atom, maintaining an infidelity below $10^{-3}$ much wider range of single-atom detunings than previous gate designs. We further show that a large fraction of residual error is dominated by population outside the computational subspace, which can be converted into heralded erasures. Our results significantly reduce the requirements on laser frequency stability and intensity homogeneity, field calibration and atomic temperature toward practical fault-tolerant quantum computing with neutral atoms.

</details>


### [101] [The Geometry of Dissipative Complexity: A Levi-Type Decomposition Theorem for Markovian Quantum Dynamics via Lie Wedges and Invariant Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27330)
*Yibin Wang,Dhara Thakkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对开放马尔可夫量子系统的控制问题，提出了动力学李楔（dynamical Lie wedge）的Levi分解与重构定理，将不可逆耗散方向纳入李代数结构分析，并证明总耗散强度仅取决于可解根坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在闭量子系统中，李代数能描述控制哈密顿量的组合方式；但在开放马尔可夫系统中，耗散引入了不可逆方向，传统李代数无法保留这些信息。动力学李楔以凸锥形式保存了局部允许生成元的信息，但缺乏相应的结构分解理论。

Method: 证明了动力学李楔的分解与重构定理：将楔分解为半单与可解两部分数据，记录耦合方式，并提供逆重构过程。基于此框架推导出生成代数的四种结构类型，并分析耗散强度与可解根坐标的关系。

Result: 建立了动力学李楔的Levi分解与重构定理；给出了生成代数的四种结构类型；证明了每个允许生成元的总耗散强度仅取决于其可解根坐标；特别地，当生成李代数为半单时，所有允许生成元均为哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 这项工作将李代数的结构方法推广到不可逆马尔可夫控制中，阐明了耗散在生成元几何中的编码方式，为开放量子系统控制提供了新的结构分析工具。

Abstract: Lie algebras describe how control Hamiltonians combine in closed quantum systems. In open Markovian systems, however, dissipation introduces irreversible directions that the Lie algebra alone does not retain. A dynamical Lie wedge preserves this information as a convex cone of locally admissible generators. The classical Levi decomposition separates a finite-dimensional Lie algebra into semisimple and solvable parts. In this work, we prove a corresponding decomposition and reconstruction theorem for dynamical Lie wedges.
  The theorem decomposes the wedge into semisimple and solvable data, records how these components are coupled, and provides a converse reconstruction of the wedge. The framework yields four structural types of the generated algebra. We further prove that the total dissipation strength of every admissible generator depends only on its coordinate in the solvable radical. In particular, if the generated Lie algebra is semisimple, every admissible generator is Hamiltonian. Together, these results extend Lie-algebraic structural methods to irreversible Markovian control and clarify how dissipation is encoded in the generator geometry.

</details>


### [102] [Krylov Break Times from an Inhomogeneous Lieb--Robinson Light Cone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27399)
*Shunji Matsuura,Yoji Kawamura,Joseph Salfi,Satoshi Iso*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Krylov截断近似在量子动力学中的有效时间尺度。作者论证该时间由Jacobi链上因果传播决定，相关距离是非均匀输运度量ρ(m,n)，并证明该度量下的Lieb-Robinson界。他们给出截断动力学的break time下界t*(m) ≥ τ_m[1-o(1)]，其中τ_m=ρ(0,m)，并在输运受限情形下证明该下界是紧的；但在探针激发局域谱部分时，break time可视为无穷。数值实验支持相关结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Krylov和Lanczos近似广泛应用于量子动力学、量子子空间方法和哈密顿学习，但一个核心实际问题是：m维Krylov截断在多大时间范围内是可信的？此前缺乏对这种近似有效时间尺度的严格刻画，本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 作者将Krylov截断的动力学映射到关联的Jacobi链，引入非均匀输运度量ρ(m,n)=Σ 1/b_j。证明该度量下的Lieb-Robinson界，通过结合Lieb-Robinson界与Duhamel公式推导截断误差的下界。分析两种情形：(1) 输运受限情形，信息传播到截断边界并折返；(2) 探针仅激发谱的局域部分，边界无信号到达。

Result: 证明break time下界t*(m;ε) ≥ τ_m[1-o(1)]，其中τ_m=ρ(0,m)；输运受限情形下该下界紧，即t*(m)≃τ_m；小权重极限给出Lieb-Robinson速度v_LR=2，传播在ρ≃2|t|锥外被指数压制。在探针激发谱局域部分时，存在状态依赖的临界Krylov维度m*，超过后break time有效为无穷。自旋链和随机Jacobi矩阵的数值测试支持结论。

Conclusion: Krylov截断的有效时间尺度由Jacobi链上的因果传播决定，其break time与输运度量τ_m紧密相关。这为量子模拟和量子子空间方法中如何选择Krylov维度提供了严格的指导原则。

Abstract: Krylov and Lanczos approximations are used in quantum dynamics, quantum subspace methods, and Hamiltonian learning. A practical question is how long an $m$-dimensional Krylov truncation can be trusted. We argue that this time is fixed by causal propagation on the associated Jacobi chain. The relevant distance is not the Krylov index itself, but the inhomogeneous transport metric $ρ(m,n) = \sum_{j=\min(m,n)}^{\max(m,n)-1} 1/b_j$, where $b_j$ is the Lanczos hopping across the bond $j \leftrightarrow j+1$. We prove a Lieb--Robinson bound in this metric. Its small-weight limit gives the velocity $v_{\rm LR} = 2$, meaning that propagation is exponentially suppressed outside the cone $ρ(m,n) \simeq 2|t|$. The error of a finite Krylov approximation to the return amplitude is a round-trip effect: information has to travel from the probe to the truncation boundary and back. Combining the Lieb--Robinson bound with Duhamel's formula yields a lower bound on the error of the truncated dynamics. For a fixed tolerance $ε$, let the break time $t_\ast(m;ε)$ denote the longest time for which the $m$-dimensional truncation is guaranteed to reproduce the exact return amplitude within error $ε$. We show that $t_\ast(m;ε) \ge τ_m[1-o(1)]$, where $τ_m = ρ(0,m) = \sum_{j<m} 1/b_j$. When the probe spreads along the chain, this lower bound is also tight, so $t_\ast(m) \simeq τ_m$. The situation is different when the probe excites only a localized part of the spectrum, or a part already resolved by the truncation. In this case, essentially no signal reaches the boundary. Beyond a state-dependent Krylov dimension $m_\ast$, the approximation can therefore remain accurate at all times, and the break time is effectively infinite. Numerical tests on spin chains and random Jacobi matrices support $t_\ast(m) \simeq τ_m$ in the transport-limited regime.

</details>


### [103] [Demystifying Relativistic Quantum Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26474)
*R. Muciño,E. Okon,D. Sudarsky,M. Wiedemann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统反驳了相对论性客观坍缩理论的种种概念性反对意见，并厘清了剩余的技术挑战，最终论证该理论为构建完全相对论性的量子框架提供了有前景的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非相对论性客观坍缩理论在解决标准量子力学的概念问题方面取得了显著成功，但将其拓展到相对论领域一直面临严重的概念异议和技术挑战，这些障碍常被用来质疑整个研究纲领的可行性。

Method: 本文采用系统性的批判分析，逐一反驳概念层面的反对意见（包括瞬时坍缩与相对论的张力、性质值和概率的参考系依赖性、超光速信号的可能性以及叙事的失败），同时为技术层面的挑战（无穷大问题、协变框架构造困难、非标准场的需求）提供结构化梳理。

Result: 作者成功反驳了概念上的主要反对意见，并将技术挑战加以系统化分类和说明，论证了这些挑战并非不可克服。

Conclusion: 相对论性坍缩理论为迈向完全相对论性的量子框架提供了一条有前景的路线，能够克服标准量子理论的概念局限。

Abstract: Non-relativistic objective collapse theories have been remarkably successful in addressing the conceptual problems of standard quantum mechanics. Despite substantial efforts, the project of extending them to the relativistic domain remains burdened by significant conceptual objections and technical challenges, often taken to cast doubt on the viability of the program as a whole. On the conceptual side, relativistic collapse theories have been claimed to face challenges involving tension between instantaneous collapse and relativity, frame-dependence of property values and probabilities, the possibility of superluminal signaling and the failure of narratability. On the technical side, persistent infinities, difficulties in constructing fully covariant frameworks and the apparent need for non-standard fields have hindered the development of workable models. In this paper, we offer a systematic rebuttal of the conceptual objections and provide a structured account of the remaining technical challenges. We conclude that relativistic collapse theories do provide a promising route toward a fully relativistic quantum framework that overcomes the conceptual limitations of standard quantum theory.

</details>


### [104] [Quantum Chaos and Quantum Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27350)
*Jordan Cotler,Felipe Hernández*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过量子最优输运理论为量子混沌建立了严格的数学基础，定义了量子李雅普诺夫指数，该指数避免了直接推广经典指数的发散问题，并在半经典极限下恢复经典全局扩张率和最大李雅普诺夫指数，同时阐明了OTOC在量子混沌诊断中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典混沌可由李雅普诺夫指数表征（测量邻近轨迹的指数发散），但直接将其推广到量子力学面临根本性困难：量子态的波动性质以及量子相空间中非对易几何阻碍了经典混沌理论的直接推广，因此需要寻找量子混沌的严格数学框架。

Method: 利用量子最优输运理论作为核心工具，该理论为测量扩展量子分布之间的距离提供了缺失的几何基础。在此基础上定义量子李雅普诺夫指数，并通过半经典极限分析证明其恢复经典的全局扩张率。

Result: 定义的量子李雅普诺夫指数避免了朴素推广经典指数时出现的发散问题；在半经典极限下可恢复经典全局扩张率，当两者一致时即得到通常的最大李雅普诺夫指数；建立了经典轨迹发散与半经典相空间演化的紧密联系；阐明了OTOC作为量子混沌诊断工具的角色。

Conclusion: 量子最优输运被确立为量子混沌理论的统一数学基础，为从简单少体模型到复杂多体系统的全范围量子动力学行为表征提供了新工具。

Abstract: Chaos in classical systems can be characterized by Lyapunov exponents that measure the exponential divergence of nearby trajectories, but directly extending this framework to quantum mechanics has been a persistent challenge. The wavelike nature of quantum states and the non-commutative geometry of quantum phase space obstruct a straightforward generalization of classical chaos theory. Here we develop a rigorous approach to quantum chaos by leveraging quantum optimal transport theory, which provides the missing geometric foundation for measuring distances between extended quantum distributions. We define quantum Lyapunov exponents that naturally avoid divergences encountered when naïvely generalizing classical exponents, and show that in the semiclassical limit they recover the classical global expansion rate, and hence the usual maximal Lyapunov exponent when they coincide. Our framework provides a tight connection between the divergence of classical trajectories and semiclassical phase space evolution, and additionally clarifies the role of out-of-time-order correlators as diagnostics of quantum chaos. These results establish quantum optimal transport as a unifying mathematical foundation for quantum chaos theory, providing new tools to characterize dynamical behavior across the full range of quantum dynamics from simple few-body models to complex many-body systems.

</details>


### [105] [Detuning- and Stark-robust Rydberg gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27426)
*Elie Bataille,Gyohei Nomura,Manuel Endres*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种更通用的双光子里德堡纠缠门框架，其中两个量子比特态都参与门操作，从而实现对抗光移（light-shift）诱导失谐误差的完全或部分鲁棒性，并展示了两种门构造方案及一个新型的"掠过式"（fly-by）里德堡纠缠门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于双光子跃迁的里德堡纠缠门因光移诱导误差随参数不利缩放而受限，且已知在仅一个量子比特态耦合到里德堡态的传统协议中不可能实现对此类失谐误差的鲁棒性，需要更通用的框架来解决这一根本限制。

Method: 作者提出了一个更通用的框架，其中两个量子比特态同时参与里德堡门操作。基于此框架设计了两种门构造：一种完全消除光移误差，另一种将误差转化为可通过局部操作校正的单比特误差。同时通过映射光移强度、强度不均匀性和里德堡衰减率等参数空间来评估这些协议的性能优势。

Result: 两种新协议在光移强度、强度不均匀性和里德堡衰减率等参数区间内均优于广泛使用的时间最优里德堡门，且这些有利条件已覆盖当前最先进实验条件。此外，还展示了"掠过式"里德堡纠缠门这一对新兴中性原子量子计算架构至关重要的原语。

Conclusion: 通过让两个量子比特态共同参与里德堡门操作，可以在更一般的设计框架中实现对抗光移误差的鲁棒性，为中性原子量子计算提供更优的门实现方案，并支持新型量子计算架构。

Abstract: Rydberg entangling gates driven by a two-photon transition in alkali atoms suffer from an adverse scaling of light-shift-induced errors. Robustness to such detuning errors is known to be impossible to achieve in the design of conventional Rydberg gate protocols, where only one of the qubit states is coupled to the Rydberg state. Here, we show that in a more general framework, in which both qubit states take part in the gate, full or partial robustness to these errors can be realized. We present two gate constructions, which either cancel the errors outright or convert them into single-qubit errors that can be corrected locally. We map the regimes -- in terms of light-shift strength, intensity inhomogeneity, and Rydberg decay rate -- in which these protocols outperform the widely used time-optimal Rydberg gate, and find that they already include the conditions of state-of-the-art experiments. Finally, we show the existence of a Rydberg `fly-by' entangling gate, an important primitive for an emerging class of neutral-atom quantum computing architectures.

</details>


### [106] [Strong Coupling of the Mo Mo Stretching Mode to a Locally Confined Stokes Raman Field in Mo2 Molecular Resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26482)
*Miao Meng,Ying Ning Tan,Guang Yuan Zhu,Chun Y. Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了二钼配合物中Mo-Mo伸缩振动与局部受限拉曼散射场之间的强耦合现象，在无腔室环境条件下观察到了Rabi型劈裂、Mollow型边带及高阶拉曼特征，表明形成了穿戴振动场态（dressed vibration field states），并提出了将金属-金属键本身作为光学场限制谐振腔的新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将光场限制在分子尺度是纳米光子学和分子量子光学的核心目标。目前实现强耦合通常需要外部光学腔室，如何在无腔室条件下实现分子尺度的光场限制和振动-光场强耦合是一个关键挑战。

Method: 在环境条件、无腔室的拉曼散射实验中，对二钼甲脒配合物（Mo2(DAniF)4和Mo2(DTolF)4）的Mo-Mo伸缩振动（约400 cm-1）进行共振拉曼光谱分析，观察Rabi劈裂、Mollow型边带和高阶拉曼特征，并用Jaynes-Cummings型耦合模型及光子数依赖关系Ωn=Ω√n解释；同时对比低极化率的Mo2(O2CCH3)4，并重新分析文献中的炔基Mo2配合物的共振拉曼光谱进行验证。

Result: 高极化率的二钼甲脒配合物表现出Rabi型劈裂和Mollow型边带，边带位移遵循Jaynes-Cummings型的光子数依赖关系；强位移拉曼特征被归属为穿戴态阶梯内的leapfrog跃迁。低极化率配合物仅显示单一Mo-Mo伸缩带，文献数据的再分析也支持该耦合框架。

Conclusion: Mo2单元同时充当拉曼活性振荡器和分子谐振腔，既能支持、限制并增强局域生成的散射场，又提供了振动-光场耦合以及化学定义的金属-金属键内光场限制的光谱学证据。

Abstract: Confining optical fields to molecular dimensions is a central objective in nanophotonics and molecular quantum optics. Here, we report strong coupling of the Mo Mo stretching vibration to a locally confined Raman scattering field in quadruply bonded dimolybdenum complexes. Under ambient, cavity-free conditions, the dimolybdenum formamidinate complexes Mo2(DAniF)4 and Mo2(DTolF)4 exhibit Rabi-type splitting, Mollow-type sidebands, and higher-order Raman features centered near the Mo Mo stretching frequency of 400 cm-1, indicating formation of dressed vibration field states. The sideband displacements from the resonance follow the photon-number-dependent relation Ωn=Ωνn, consistent with Jaynes Cummings-type coupling, while strongly displaced Raman features are assigned to leapfrog transitions within the same dressed-state ladder. In contrast, the less polarizable Mo2(O2CCH3)4 complex exhibits essentially a single Mo Mo stretching band. Reanalysis of reported resonance Raman spectra of an alkynyl Mo2 complex further supports this coupling framework. These results suggest that the Mo2 unit simultaneously serves as the Raman active oscillator and the molecular resonator that supports, confines, and enhances the locally generated scattering field, providing spectroscopic evidence for vibration field coupling and optical-field confinement within a chemically defined metal metal bond.

</details>


### [107] [On supporting affine functionals for Entanglement of Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27363)
*A. S. Holevo,M. E. Shirokov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出一个常见错误假设：EoF（纠缠熵）的凸包结构与子系统有限维并不能保证任何状态下全局支撑仿射泛函的存在性。作者用Wootters公式构造了一个两比特系统的反例，证明了当状态ρ退化时该性质不成立。此外，文章还给出了有限维和无限维双粒子量子系统中，给定状态下局部和全局支撑仿射泛函存在的条件，并据此推导出EoF在有限秩状态处的Lipschitz下半连续界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多篇文献错误地假设EoF在任意状态下都存在全局支撑仿射泛函，这一假设对于证明EoF的某些性质（如连续性、可加性等）至关重要。作者旨在纠正这一错误，提供严格的反例，并给出该性质成立的精确条件。

Method: 利用Wootters公式计算两比特系统的EoF，结合"支撑仿射泛函存在性与Lipschitz下半连续性等价"这一关键事实，通过Claude Fable 5辅助搜索构造出退化状态ρ的反例。同时，从数学上推导出局部和全局支撑仿射泛函存在的充要条件，并应用于有限秩状态的Lipschitz下半连续界估计。

Result: 构造了一个两比特系统中的显式反例，证明退化状态下全局支撑仿射泛函不一定存在，推翻了此前文献中的假设。同时给出了局部和全局支撑仿射泛函存在的条件，并基于这些条件得到了EoF在有限秩状态处的Lipschitz下半连续界（形式为E_F(ρ)-E_F(σ)≤C_ρ‖ρ-σ‖₁），包括有无支持限制的两种情形。

Conclusion: EoF的凸包结构和系统有限维性不足以保证退化状态处全局支撑仿射泛函的存在性；需要通过Lipschitz下半连续性来判断。作者提供的条件和界限为EoF的深入研究提供了更精确的理论工具。

Abstract: In several articles, the authors assume that the convex roof structure of the EoF and finite-dimensionality of subsystems $A$ and $B$ guarantee the existence of the (global) supporting affine functional for the EoF at any state of the system $AB$. This means that for any state $ρ$ of $AB$ there is a Hermitian operator $Λ_ρ$ on $\mathcal{H}_{AB}=\mathcal{H}_A\otimes\mathcal{H}_B$ such that $E_F(ρ)=\mathrm{Tr}Λ_ρρ$ and $E_F(σ)\geq\mathrm{Tr}Λ_ρσ$ for any state $σ$ of $AB$. We present an explicit example showing that, when $ρ$ is degenerate, this is not true even in the simplest case when $A$ and $B$ are qubit systems. The construction is based on the fact that the existence of a supporting affine functional for the EoF at a state $ρ$ is equivalent to the Lipschitz lower semicontinuity of the EoF at this state $ρ$. We use Wootters' formula and the help of Claude Fable 5 to find a state $ρ$ of the system $AB$ for which the latter property does not hold.
  We also describe conditions for the existence the local and global supporting affine functionals for the EoF at a given state of both finite and infinite-dimensional bipartite quantum systems. These conditions allow us to find Lipschitz lower semicontinuity bounds for the EoF at a given finite rank state $ρ$ (i.e. inequalities of the form $\,E_F(ρ)-E_F(σ)\leq C_ρ\|ρ-σ\|_1$) with and without restrictions on the support of the state $σ$.

</details>


### [108] [Dynamics of local quantum information in random unitary circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27440)
*Ratul Thakur,Sthitadhi Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一套基于"信息格点"框架的理论，用于解析局部随机酉电路中的局部量子信息动力学。研究发现信息在不同长度尺度上的分布遵循Tracy-Widom形式，随时间以t线性弹道运动向更大尺度移动，伴随约t^(1/3)的展宽，且该行为在Haar随机电路和随机Clifford电路两种不同设定下具有普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的信息置乱表征（如二分纠缠或算子增长）本质上是粗粒度的，无法揭示量子信息在系统不同区域和不同长度尺度之间流动的时空细观结构。作者旨在建立一种能够分辨局部量子信息流动的细粒度微观结构的理论。

Method: 利用信息格点（information lattice）框架系统化描述各子系统内每个长度尺度上的信息含量，识别信息驻留的长度尺度分布并研究其动力学。对具有无限局域希尔伯特空间维数的Haar随机电路，将该动力学映射为精确的经典随机过程；对作用于量子比特的随机Clifford电路，发展了稳定子生成元动力学的一个唯象Markov过程。

Result: 信息分布由Tracy-Widom形式描述，随时间以∝t的弹道速度向更大尺度移动，伴随~t^(1/3)的展宽。Haar随机电路与随机Clifford电路均表现出相同的定性行为，提示该结果具有普适性。Clifford电路中的唯象Markov过程进一步确认了Tracy-Widom分布及t^(1/3)涨落标度。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了局部量子信息在长度尺度分辨方式下的普适动力学性质，为连接精确的微观信息动力学理论与涌现的信息流流体力学描述提供了可能途径。

Abstract: The physics of information scrambling in quantum many-body systems is intimately related to thermalisation and emergence of chaos. However, its standard characterisation through bipartite entanglement or operator growth remains inherently coarse-grained, obscuring the spatiotemporal anatomy of how quantum information flows between different regions of the system and across various length scales. In this work, we develop a theory for the dynamics of local information in local random unitary circuits which resolves the fine-grained microscopic structures of local information flow in such systems. Using the framework of information lattice which systematises the information content at each length scale within each subsystem, we identify a distribution of length scales at which information resides and obtain the dynamics of the distribution. For Haar-random circuits with infinite local Hilbert-space dimensions, we map this dynamics onto an exact classical stochastic process which reveals that this distribution is described by a Tracy-Widom form which moves ballistically in time ($ \propto t$) toward larger scales, accompanied by a $\sim t^{1/3}$ broadening. We also find the same qualitative behaviour for random Clifford circuits acting on qubits. The similar scaling behaviour in two rather different settings hints strongly towards the universality of our results. In the case of Clifford circuits, we develop a phenomenological Markov process for the dynamics of the stabiliser generators in a specific gauge which confirms the Tracy-Widom distribution and the $t^{1/3}$ scaling of the fluctuations. Ultimately, our results establish the universal properties of the dynamics of local quantum information in a length scale-resolved fashion and provide a possible route towards bridging exact microscopic theories for information dynamics with emergent hydrodynamic descriptions of information flow.

</details>


### [109] [Real-Space Analysis of Two-Photon Polarization States in Type-II SPDC for High-Purity Polarization Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26499)
*Koji Taninaka,Takashi Kakue,Ken Morita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过计算II型自发参量下转换（SPDC）产生的双光子波函数的实空间分布，识别偏振与实空间坐标之间的空间关联方向，并据此设计沿该关联方向的孔径形状。数值模拟和实验均证明，这种"关联对齐"的孔径能同时提高纠缠纯度和光子收集效率，为优化高纯度高亮度纠缠光子源提供了实用设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: II型SPDC产生的双光子波函数因双折射效应而具有空间与偏振自由度之间的复杂耦合，这种耦合会影响纠缠纯度和收集效率。现有研究缺乏从实空间双光子波函数出发系统设计孔径形状的方法，因此需要一种能够同时优化纯度和效率的孔径设计策略。

Method: 首先计算BBO晶体中II型SPDC产生的双光子波函数的实空间分布，识别偏振态与实空间坐标之间的空间关联方向；然后根据这一关联方向设计特定形状的孔径以限制沿该方向的收集；最后通过数值模拟和实验验证所设计孔径的性能。

Result: 数值模拟和实验均证明，沿偏振-空间关联方向设计的孔径能够同时实现更高的纠缠纯度和更高的光子收集效率，优于传统的对称或圆形孔径设计。

Conclusion: 基于实空间双光子波函数优化孔径几何形状是一种有效的实用设计原则，为实现高纯度、高亮度的SPDC纠缠光子源提供了新途径。

Abstract: Spontaneous parametric down-conversion (SPDC) is one of the most widely used sources of polarization-entangled photon pairs, and understanding the generated biphoton state is essential for realizing high-brightness and high-purity entangled-photon sources. In particular, Type-II SPDC produces a biphoton wavefunction with a complex coupling between the spatial and polarization degrees of freedom owing to birefringence. In this study, we calculate the real-space distribution of the two-photon polarization state from the biphoton wavefunction of Type-II SPDC generated in a \b{eta}-barium borate (BBO) crystal. By identifying the spatial correlation direction between polarization and real-space coordinates, we designed aperture shapes that restrict the collection along this correlation direction. Through both numerical simulations and experiments, we demonstrate that such correlation-aligned apertures simultaneously achieve higher entanglement purity and improved photon collection efficiency. These results establish a practical design principle for optimizing aperture geometries based on the real-space biphoton wavefunction, providing a new approach to realizing high-purity and high-brightness SPDC entangled-photon sources.

</details>


### [110] [Spectral Fingerprints of Gauge Theories on a Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27457)
*Graham Van Goffrier,Debasish Banerjee,Bipasha Chakraborty,Emilie Huffman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种将最大混合态谱采样算法聚焦到光谱任意区域的方法，用于提取量子多体模型（如非阿贝尔SO(3)规范理论）的完整谱指纹，涵盖从基态量子临界到热化行为的物理信息，并在近-term超导量子硬件上进行了78层双量子比特门深度的演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子谱采样是提取哈密顿量能谱信息的强力工具，但标准算法覆盖整个能谱范围，分辨率有限。作者希望将该算法聚焦到光谱的特定区域，从而能够同时研究基态物理（如量子临界性）和热化行为（如态密度），并为近-term量子硬件上的实际应用提供可行路径。

Method: 论文方法包括：(1) 将最大化混合态谱采样聚焦到目标光谱区域； (2) 在(1+1)d非阿贝尔SO(3)规范理论上进行演示； (3) 提出一种基于Pauli-frame优化的受控时间演化解编策略； (4) 利用动态电路能力将所需量子比特数大致减半； (5) 说明量子读出误差缓解在该方法中自然简化； (6) 展示两个物理应用（无序多体重转变和中谱态密度）及其所需的后期处理步骤。

Result: 在近-term超导量子硬件上，使用78层双量子比特门深度的电路成功演示了该算法。通过动态电路的能力将所需量子比特数减少约一半，并受益于量子读出误差缓解的简化。同时验证了两个物理应用：无序多体重转变和中谱态密度的提取。

Conclusion: 该工作表明最大化混合态谱采样经聚焦后可成为研究量子多体系统完整能谱指纹的多功能工具，能够覆盖从基态到热化行为的物理现象，并且与近-term超导硬件兼容，为近期量子硬件上的谱学应用开辟了实际路径。

Abstract: Maximally mixed state spectral sampling is an unbiased quantum algorithm that allows for extraction of a finite-resolution spectral distribution from a Hamiltonian over potentially the entire allowed range of energies. We show how it may be focused on any desired area of the spectrum in order to learn about the full \textit{fingerprint} of the model of interest: from its ground state phenomena such as quantum criticality, obtained from the lowest lying energies, to its thermalization behavior, obtained from the mid-spectrum. We demonstrate this technique specifically on a $(1+1)d$ non-Abelian $SO(3)$ gauge theory, providing a comprehensive analysis of the steps necessary for performing this algorithm, as well as what is possible in the near-term with superconducting quantum hardware, performing simulations with circuits that are $78$ two-qubit gates deep. We show how this algorithm is able to take advantage of emerging dynamical circuit capabilities in near-term hardware to roughly halve the number required qubits, as well as how quantum readout error mitigation is trivial for this method. Along the way, we propose a novel strategy for compiling the controlled-time evolutions needed for spectral sampling by means of Pauli-frame optimizations. We illustrate two physical applications of quantum spectral sampling -- disordered many-body transitions, and mid-spectrum densities of states -- and what postprocessing steps they require beyond the Fourier outputs of the algorithm.

</details>


### [111] [Auditing Structured Randomness for Quantum Error Correction under a Bounded Cloud Fault Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26600)
*Ziqing Guo,Anthony Lawrence,Renyu Wang,Randy Kuang,Ziwen Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了云量子处理器中量子纠错电路的编码器重播种（reseeding）策略，通过定义"可接受逻辑扰动"度量，量化了每轮编码器重播种对抗故障注入攻击的安全增益，实验显示可降低86.7%的平均可接受逻辑扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云量子处理器将提交的量子纠错电路与不可信工作负载共置，固定公开编码器为故障注入攻击者提供了可重复利用的目标。每轮重播种（reseeding）可以改变物理到逻辑的故障映射，但精确的Haar随机编码器具有指数级电路成本，而高效的随机编码器集合仅提供平均矩保证，其最坏情况下的接受损坏未被刻画。因此需要一种能够量化重播种完整性增益的度量方法。

Method: 定义了一种新的度量——"可接受逻辑扰动"（accepted logical disturbance），即接受结果中有害逻辑作用的接受加权测度，并推导其精确的Haar期望值。使用多项式开销的种子化Clifford编码器族，结合稠密线性代数和门级稳定子仿真，评估在编码器已知前或已知后选择故障的两种攻击场景。

Result: 重播种将学习每个编码器后选择故障的平均可接受逻辑扰动从0.150降低到编码器已知前选择单一故障时的0.020，实现了86.7%的降低，该降低由拒绝（rejection）机制实现。固定距离三的[[5,1,3]]码可纠正所有测试的重量一泡利错误，而所选编码器族中18.5%的采样编码器满足精确量子纠错条件。

Conclusion: 实测的降低量化了重播种带来的完整性增益，并在明确的故障和攻击者知识模型下，将事后选择检测（postselected detection）与精确纠错（exact correction）区分开来，为云量子处理器的安全编码器设计提供了量化依据。

Abstract: Cloud quantum processors compile submitted quantum error correction circuits and may colocate them with untrusted workloads. A fixed public encoder gives a fault-injection adversary a reusable target. Per-run reseeding changes the physical-to-logical fault map. Exact Haar-random encoders have exponential circuit cost. Efficient random ensembles provide average-moment guarantees and leave worst-case accepted corruption uncharacterized. We define accepted logical disturbance, an acceptance-weighted measure of harmful logical action in accepted results, and derive its exact Haar expectation. We evaluate a polynomial-cost seeded Clifford encoder family using dense linear algebra and gate-level stabilizer simulation against faults chosen before or after the encoder is known. Reseeding reduces mean accepted logical disturbance from 0.150 for faults chosen after learning each encoder to 0.020 for one fault chosen before it is known. The 86.7% reduction results from rejection. The fixed distance-three \([[5,1,3]]\) code corrects every tested weight-one Pauli, while 18.5% of sampled encoders in the selected ensemble satisfy exact quantum error correction. The measured reduction quantifies the integrity gain from reseeding and separates postselected detection from exact correction under explicit fault and attacker-knowledge models.

</details>


### [112] [Quantum-enhanced single and multiparameter metrology in qutrit ensembles by generalized twisting dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26630)
*Deep Datta,Sayam Chakraborty,John Drew Wilson,Vaibhav Madhok,Athreya Shankar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于三能级系统（qutrit）集合的量子增强传感，提出了三种实验上可行的OAT（单轴扭转）模型泛化，发现三种模型在编码方向上有很大自由度（6/8方向接近海森堡极限），并通过时间反转协议实现实验访问，同时解决了估计模糊性和局域耗散问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子增强传感研究主要集中在qubit（二能级）协议上，但许多实验平台已具备相干控制和纠缠多能级系统的能力。将量子增强传感推广到qutrit（三能级）系统，可以探索超越SU(2)代数限制的新可能性，例如利用su(3)代数实现qubit系统中不存在的计量学优势。

Method: 提出了三种qutrit泛化的OAT模型，其中纠缠操作仅涉及两个能级，第三个能级作为旁观者。利用量子Fisher信息工具包表征动态生成态的计量学效用，通过数值模拟分析编码方向的自由度，设计有效时间反转协议以实现实验访问，并研究了估计模糊性和局域耗散问题的优化策略。

Result: 三种模型均提供相当大的编码方向自由度，短时间演化后8个可能方向中有6个展现接近海森堡极限的缩放。通过优化传感器工作点可克服估计模糊性和局域耗散问题。多参数同时估计时的测量不相容性在零工作点随系统尺寸增大而受到抑制。其中一种模型可实现一对对易生成元的近海森堡极限计量，这一可能性源于su(3)代数，在基于qubit集合的SU(2)旋转传感器中不存在。

Conclusion: qutrit集合能够提供相比qubit协议更丰富的量子增强传感能力，特别是在编码方向自由度和多参数估计方面具有独特优势。su(3)代数结构使得对易生成元也能实现近海森堡极限的测量，拓展了量子计量学的理论边界。

Abstract: Quantum-enhanced sensing with atomic ensembles has predominantly focused on qubit-based protocols, despite the growing ability of many experimental platforms to coherently control and entangle multi-level systems. Here, we investigate quantum-enhanced sensing with qutrit ensembles by introducing three experimentally feasible qutrit generalisations of the one-axis twisting (OAT) model that involve entangling operations only between two levels, while the third level primarily acts as a spectator. We characterize the metrological utility of the dynamically generated states using the quantum Fisher information toolbox. We find numerically that all three models offer considerable freedom in encoding direction for quantum-enhanced sensing, with up to 6 out of 8 possible directions exhibit near-Heisenberg scaling after a short evolution time. We discuss experimental access to this enhanced metrological precision via effective time-reversal protocols. Furthermore, we examine the practical issues of estimation ambiguity and local dissipation, and show that they can be largely overcome by optimizing the sensor operating point. Finally, we show that the measurement incompatibility in estimating multiple parameters simultaneously encoded in different directions with near-Heisenberg scaling of precision is suppressed at the zero operating point as the system size increases. In the process, we find that one of the models enables near-Heisenberg scaling metrology with a pair of commuting generators, a possibility that arises from the $su(3)$ algebra and is thus absent in qubit-ensemble based sensors of collective $SU(2)$ rotations.

</details>


### [113] [Quantum estimation theory with quantum noise control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26635)
*Osamu Hirota,Masaki Sohma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Monras和Paris的量子估计理论成果，通过利用约半世纪前提出的广义外差探测概念，展示了其方案能够满足下一代量子信息机制对量子优势和实时处理能力的严格要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子估计理论的应用研究长期进展有限，但21世纪以来活跃的讨论和提案推动了其应用发展。下一代量子信息机制亟需保证经典理论无法实现的量子优势以及无延迟的严格实时处理，这为扩展Monras和Paris的原始思想提供了迫切的实际需求。

Method: 利用约半个世纪前提出的广义外差探测概念，基于Monras和Paris的理论成果构建方案，以同时满足量子优势保证和实时处理需求。

Result: 该方案能够有效满足近未来信息技术对量子优势和严格实时处理的严格要求。

Conclusion: 广义外差检测与Monras和Paris理论结果的结合为量子估计理论的实用化提供了可行方向，能够应对下一代量子信息技术的迫切需求。

Abstract: Quantum estimation theory serves as one of fundamental backbones of quantum Shannon information theory and was mathematically systematized by pioneers of quantum information science. Although its applied research saw limited progress for a long period, the 21st century has witnessed active discussions and numerous fruitful proposals regarding its applications. Notably, the contributions of Monras and Paris represent one of the most remarkable achievements addressing the realization problem of estimation bounds. This paper aims to demonstrate a practical direction for extending their original ideas. Next-generation quantum information mechanisms urgently demand guarantees of quantum advantage unattainable by classical theory, alongside strict real-time processing without delays. By leveraging the concept of generalized heterodyne detection proposed nearly half a century ago, we demonstrate that a scheme based on their results can effectively meet the stringent requirements of near-future information technologies.

</details>


### [114] [A thermometric quantum Brownian model for low-temperature electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26646)
*Harry T. H. Fung,Thomas M. Stace*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子布朗运动方程（QBME）的电阻测温模型，以替代量子光学主方程（QOME）在量子电子学中建模电阻的方法，并通过transmon与电阻并联的简单例子对比了两者的预测差异及实验判别方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QOME在量子电子学中被广泛用于建模电阻，但它忽视了一些基本物理性质，限制了对某些超导现象的建模能力，因此需要一种更完善的模型来替代。

Method: 从QBME出发，构建了一种测温（thermometric）形式的电阻模型，使其适用于低温下耗散电子器件的实际建模；并以transmon并联电阻为例，将测温QBME的预测与QOME进行对比。

Result: 研究表明，QBME与QOME在示例中给出了可比较但物理上不同的预测结果，并提出了可用于判别两种模型的潜在实验测试方案。

Conclusion: 测温QBME为量子耗散电子学提供了一种更为严谨的建模框架，其与QOME的预测差异可通过特定实验加以判别，具有重要意义。

Abstract: Quantum mechanical models of resistors in quantum electronics are often based on the quantum optical master equation (QOME). The QOME overlooks several fundamental properties, limiting its ability to model certain superconducting phenomena. Here we present a thermometric model of resistors, adapted from the quantum Brownian motion equation (QBME), that facilitates practical use of the QBME in modelling dissipative electronics at low temperatures. We compare the thermometric QBME presented here with predictions of the QOME, in the simple example of a transmon shunted by a resistor. We show that both the QBME and QOME yield comparable but physically distinct predictions, and discuss potential experimental tests with which to discriminate their use in modelling practical experiments.

</details>


### [115] [Observing relativistic trajectories of single photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26778)
*Sayantan Das,Daniel S. Dahl,Daniel Peace,Marcelo P. Almeida,Abhishek Roy,Markus Rambach,Andrew G. White,Timothy C. Ralph,Jacquiline Romero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Michelson-Sagnac干涉仪中实验重建了单光子的相对论性Bohmian轨迹，观测到光子穿过干涉条纹时轨迹的亚光速和超光速特征，为相对论性Bohmian力学的实验探索提供了途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量子力学解释不赋予粒子确定轨迹，而Bohmian诠释赋予轨迹，但Bohmian力学与相对论的调和仅在最近才提出操作性方法。本研究旨在实验上验证和探索相对论性Bohmian力学的非直觉性质。

Method: 在Michelson-Sagnac干涉仪中，使反向传播的概率幅以光速正面干涉，通过实验方法重建单个光子的相对论性Bohmian轨迹。

Result: 观测到光子穿过干涉条纹时Bohmian轨迹同时呈现亚光速和超光速特征，与相对论性Bohmian理论的预测一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为相对论性Bohmian力学提供了实验访问途径，使其不寻常且反直觉的性质得以实验探索。

Abstract: While the standard interpretation of quantum mechanics does not assign definite trajectories to particles, the Bohmian interpretation does. Only recently has an operational method for reconciling Bohmian mechanics with relativity been proposed. Here, we experimentally reconstruct relativistic Bohmian trajectories of a single photon in a Michelson-Sagnac interferometer, where counter-propagating probability amplitudes interfere head-on at the speed of light. As predicted by the relativistic Bohmian theory, we observe subluminal and superluminal features of the Bohmian trajectories of the photon traversing through the fringes. Our work provides experimental access to relativistic Bohmian mechanics and enables exploration of its unusual and counterintuitive properties.

</details>


### [116] [Uncertainty limits for post-selected metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26781)
*Ming Ji,Holger F. Hofmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了后选择计量学中的量子Fisher信息（QFI）与Ozawa-Hall条件不确定性之间的对应关系，解释了后选择计量学中异常高的灵敏度来源于低概率后选择结果对生成子不确定性的集中效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后选择计量学能够实现超出常规极限的灵敏度，但其物理机制尚未完全清晰。作者希望通过将后选择QFI与量子测量理论中已知的Ozawa-Hall不确定性联系起来，从理论上阐明这种灵敏度的增强来源。

Method: 作者从量子Fisher信息的数学框架出发，建立了后选择情况下条件生成子不确定性与Ozawa-Hall不确定性之间的等价关系，并通过分析后选择测量结果如何根据其量子统计特性更新生成子不确定性，来解释QFI增强的原理。

Result: 研究表明，后选择QFI所定义的条件生成子不确定性恰好对应量子测量理论中的Ozawa-Hall不确定性。QFI能够超越生成子本征值的最大不确定性的关键在于，后选择倾向于将初始生成子不确定性的大部分集中在低概率的后选择测量结果中，从而产生异常的条件不确定性。

Conclusion: 后选择计量学中异常灵敏度的根本来源是异常条件不确定性（Ozawa-Hall不确定性），这为理解后选择增强量子计量灵敏度的机制提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: For unitary transformations, the quantum Fisher information (QFI) of a pure state is given by the uncertainty of the generator in that state. In post-selected metrology, the QFI is given by a modified expression describing conditional quantum statistics of the generator. Here, we show that the conditional generator uncertainties defined by post-selected QFI correspond to Ozawa-Hall uncertainties known from the theoretical analysis of quantum measurements. The post-selected measurement outcome updates the generator uncertainty according to the quantum statistics of that outcome.
