### [139] [A proof of the Arnold-Givental conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27242)
*Shaoyun Bai,Egor Shelukhin,Yi Wang,Guangbo Xu*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文完整证明了Arnold-Givental猜想：对于闭辛流形上的反辛对合，任意Hamiltonian微分同胚将不动集L拉回后与L横截相交，交点数量至少为L的F₂系数同调维数。证明结合了积分Floer理论、Lu的约化方法以及为问题量身定制的Z/2等变Floer理论中的局域化技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arnold-Givental猜想是辛几何中长期悬而未决的核心问题，它将Lagrangian交点的下界与不动集的拓扑信息（F₂系数的总同调维数）联系起来，推广了经典的Arnold猜想，对理解反辛对合下Lagrangian交理论具有重要意义。

Method: 证明方法综合了三条技术路径：(1) 第一和第四作者发展的积分Floer理论；(2) Lu提出的约化到Hamiltonian Floer上同调的方法；(3) 一个针对该问题量身设计的新颖思路——在Z/2等变Floer理论中引入局域化技术。

Result: 在完全一般性条件下证明了Arnold-Givental猜想，即对任意闭辛流形上的反辛对合，横截相交时满足 #(φ(L) ∩ L) ≥ dim_{F₂} H_*(L; F₂)。

Conclusion: 该结果首次在无任何额外假设的条件下建立了Arnold-Givental不等式，为Lagrangian交理论提供了强有力的全局下界，展示了积分Floer理论与等变局域化方法在解决辛几何经典猜想中的有效性。

Abstract: We prove the Arnold-Givental conjecture in full generality: given a closed symplectic manifold $(X, ω)$, an anti-symplectic involution $τ_X: X \to X$ with fixed point set $L={\rm Fix}(τ_X)$, and a Hamiltonian diffeomorphism $φ: X \to X$ such that $φ(L)$ intersects transversely with $L$, the following inequality holds: \[ \# \big( φ(L) \cap L \big) \geq {\mathrm dim}_{{\mathbb F}_2} H_*(L; {\mathbb F}_2).\] The proof combines the methods of integral Floer theory of the first and fourth authors, a reduction to Hamiltonian Floer cohomology due to Lu, and a new idea related to localization in a $\mathbb Z/2$-equivariant Floer theory tailored to the problem.

</details>


### [140] [Non-decomposable Lagrangian endoconcordances and Khovanov homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27316)
*Roman Golovko*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文构造了Legendrian纽结的不可分解Lagrangian端向同痕（endoconcordance），证明了在标准接触3-球面中，对任意正整数k，存在（稳定化的）Legendrian纽结和Lagrangian可填充的Legendrian纽结，每个至少拥有k个两两Hamiltonian非同伦（相对边界）、不可分解的Lagrangian端向同痕。不可分解性的障碍基于Khovanov同调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Legendrian纽结的Lagrangian端向同痕的可分解性，探索这类几何对象的结构丰富性，并建立基于Khovanov同调的新的不可分解性障碍。

Method: 通过构造具体的Legendrian纽结对，利用Khovanov同调作为不变性工具，证明所构造的Lagrangian端向同痕是不可分解的，并利用Hamiltonian非同伦性区分不同的端向同痕类。

Result: 对于任意正整数k，在标准接触3-球面中找到了（稳定化）Legendrian纽结和Lagrangian可填充Legendrian纽结，每个都至少有k个两两Hamiltonian非同伦（相对边界）、不可分解的Lagrangian端向同痕。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了Legendrian纽结的Lagrangian端向同痕空间的丰富结构，并表明Khovanov同调可作为区分不可分解端向同痕的有效工具。

Abstract: In this note, we construct non-decomposable Lagrangian endoconcordances of Legendrian knots. We prove that for any positive integer k, there exist a (stabilised) Legendrian knot and a Lagrangian fillable Legendrian knot in the standard contact 3-sphere, each admitting at least k pairwise Hamiltonian non-isotopic relative boundary, non-decomposable Lagrangian endoconcordances. The obstruction to decomposability that we rely on is based on Khovanov homology.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Classical and Hybrid Quantum Machine Learning for Trigger-Like Event Selection on CMS Open Data: An Eight-Qubit, PCA-Constrained Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26224)
*Tariq Mahmood,Muhammad Awais Rafique,Talab Hussain,Juan Pablo Perez Aguilar,Alfredo Raya,Muhammad Ahsan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究在高能物理触发类二分类任务上（基于CMS开放数据），对比了四种经典机器学习模型（SVM、ANN、CNN、LSTM）与四种混合量子模型（量子核、量子神经网络、量子卷积网络、量子循环网络），在8量子比特和16特征压缩的固定资源预算下进行公平比较。最强经典模型ANN达到93.53%准确率和0.9819 ROC-AUC，最强量子模型QCNN达到90.89%准确率和0.9731 ROC-AUC，训练型混合量子嵌入表现优于量子核与量子循环方法，但本研究仅作为受控参考点而非量子优势的声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理中的事件触发需要在极低的延迟和带宽预算下从海量背景中保留稀有目标事件，是机器学习应用的核心场景。现有研究缺乏在统一资源预算和一致预处理条件下对经典与量子模型的系统性公平对比，本文旨在填补这一空白，提供一个受控的基准参考。

Method: 使用CMS开放数据构建基于不变质量窗口的触发类二分类任务，输入融合重建运动学和物理驱动导出变量（伪快度差、环绕方位角差、角距离、总横动量）。量子模型在8量子比特、主成分压缩至16特征和态矢量模拟的固定预算下运行。所有模型共享相同的分层分割、预处理和决策阈值，通过准确率、ROC-AUC、F1分数、精确率和召回率五项指标评估性能。

Result: 经典模型中ANN表现最优（93.53%准确率，0.9819 ROC-AUC），量子模型中QCNN表现最优（90.89%准确率，0.9731 ROC-AUC），QNN紧随其后。量子核和量子循环方法性能落后，表明在该资源预算下可训练混合量子嵌入更具优势。

Conclusion: 在固定资源预算下，经典ANN仍略优于最佳量子模型，但量子卷积网络的表现差距较小（约2.6个百分点准确率），可训练的混合量子嵌入架构优于量子核与量子循环方法。该研究定位为受控对照基准而非量子优势声明，为后续在更大量子资源下探索量子触发模型提供了参考框架。

Abstract: Event triggering sits at the heart of high-energy physics, where the rare events of interest must be retained while an overwhelming background is discarded under tight latency and bandwidth budgets. This work compares four classical machine learning models, namely a support vector machine, an artificial neural network, a convolutional network and a long short-term memory network, with four hybrid quantum counterparts, on a trigger-like binary classification task built from CMS open data. The label is defined by an invariant-mass window, and the inputs combine reconstructed kinematics with physics-motivated derived variables: the pseudorapidity difference, the wrapped azimuthal difference, the angular separation and the total transverse momentum. The quantum models run under a fixed resource budget of eight qubits, a principal-component compression to sixteen features and state-vector simulation. Every model shares the same stratified split, the same preprocessing and a common decision threshold, and performance is reported through accuracy, ROC-AUC, F1-score, precision and recall. The strongest classical model is the artificial neural network, at 93.53 percent accuracy and 0.9819 ROC-AUC, while the strongest quantum model is the quantum convolutional network, at 90.89 percent accuracy and 0.9731 ROC-AUC, with the quantum neural network close behind. The quantum-kernel and recurrent quantum approaches trail both, which places the trainable hybrid embeddings ahead within this budget. The study is meant as a controlled reference point rather than a claim of quantum advantage.

</details>


### [142] [FlaSR: A Mathematica Package for the Automatic Generation of $U$-spin Sum Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26248)
*Margarita Gavrilova,Livia Kong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍FlaSR，一个基于Mathematica的软件包，用于自动生成高阶振幅和振幅平方的SU(2)味求和规则，特别关注U-spin对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 味求和规则在粒子物理中具有重要作用，但传统上需要人工推导，过程繁琐且容易出错。基于对味求和规则数学结构的新理解，作者开发了自动化工具以高效生成这些规则。

Method: 该软件包以粒子多重态列表和时间反演对称极限哈密顿量的变换性质为输入，自动生成完整过程列表，并输出振幅求和规则（在所有对称破缺阶生成完整集合）和振幅平方求和规则（当前版本不保证生成完整集合）。

Result: 通过一系列代表性实例展示了软件包的功能范围，能够处理由对称性相关过程构成的系统，特别适用于U-spin相关的分析。

Conclusion: FlaSR为味求和规则的自动生成提供了高效工具，振幅求和规则可在所有阶完全生成，而振幅平方求和规则的完整性保证是未来改进方向。

Abstract: We present FlaSR, a Mathematica package for the automatic generation of higher-order amplitude and amplitude-squared $SU(2)$ flavor sum rules, with a particular emphasis on $U$-spin. The package is based on recent insights into the mathematical structure of flavor sum rules. Given a system of symmetry-related processes defined by a list of particle multiplets in the initial and final states, together with the transformation properties of the symmetry-limit Hamiltonian, the package generates the full list of processes and outputs amplitude and amplitude-squared sum rules for the system. For amplitude sum rules, the package generates the complete set at all orders in symmetry breaking for which such relations exist. For amplitude-squared sum rules, which translate directly into sum rules among physical observables, the current version of the package is not guaranteed to generate the complete set. We illustrate the scope of the package with a range of representative examples.

</details>


### [143] [OperatorToC++: Transpiling Matched EFT Coefficients to Low-Level Routines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26254)
*Sabine Kraml,Andre Lessa,Suraj Prakash,Felix Wilsch*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为OperatorToC++的混合工具，它结合Mathematica和C++的优势，处理从新物理模型匹配到低能有效场论后的解析表达式，将其翻译打包为C++类/函数，并支持Python调用，以便进行后续数值分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动匹配新物理模型参数与低能有效场论方面已有显著进展，但匹配之后的分析步骤（如处理复杂循环函数、涉及张量对象的长求和与乘积）仍然困难，缺少一个高效便捷的工具来桥接解析匹配结果与数值分析。

Method: OperatorToC++是一个可扩展的混合工具，结合Mathematica（处理符号解析推导）和C++（高性能计算）的优势。它先处理匹配表达式中复杂的循环函数和张量求和/乘积，然后将结果翻译并打包为C++类和函数，形成便于数值分析的平台，并支持将编译后的Wilson系数方法作为Python函数调用。

Result: 该工具能够高效处理匹配后的解析表达式中的复杂性，生成可被进一步数值分析的C++代码，并通过Python绑定与高能物理工作流中的Python库生态连接。

Conclusion: OperatorToC++填补了自动匹配工具链中"匹配后分析"环节的空白，通过Mathematica-C++混合架构和Python接口，为高能物理理论研究提供了从解析匹配到数值计算的完整、便捷解决方案。

Abstract: In recent years, significant progress has been made in the development of automated tools that match the parameters of new physics models and the appropriate low-energy Effective Field Theories. This work introduces an extensible, hybrid tool, OperatorToC++, that combines the strengths of Mathematica and C++ to facilitate the next steps beyond the matching. OperatorToC++ efficiently tackles the complexities within the analytical matched expressions such as intricate loop-functions and lengthy sums and products involving tensor objects. It then translates and bundles the results into C++ classes and functions which provide a convenient platform for further numerical analyses. Finally, it offers the possibility of calling the compiled Wilson coefficient methods as Python functions, thus enabling to link them with the vast Python library ecosystem for High Energy Physics workflows.

</details>


### [144] [First-Principles Nuclear Modeling for Light Dark Matter Experiments at the Intensity Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26255)
*Taylor R. Gray,Alberto Scalesi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用多体ab initio方法（结合手征有效场论相互作用）计算电子固定靶实验中轻暗物质中介子产生信号率，针对20Ne、34Si和56Fe三种核，考虑弹性和准弹性散射，发现准弹性处理可比常用唯象参数化提高信号产额两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强度前沿加速器实验是探测轻暗物质范式的有力工具，但可靠的信号预测需要对靶核进行精确建模。现有常用唯象参数化方法可能不够精确，因此需要更精确的多体微观方法。

Method: 采用多体ab initio方法，基于手征有效场论相互作用，计算轻暗物质中介子产生信号率。利用ab initio核弹性形状因子和谱函数，通过蒙特卡洛事件生成器实现，涵盖弹性和准弹性散射两种过程，对20Ne、34Si和56Fe三种代表性核在多个电子束能量下计算截面。

Result: 与常用唯象参数化相比，准弹性处理使信号产额提高最多达两个数量级；对于较轻的中介子，两种方法结果一致。

Conclusion: ab initio方法（特别是准弹性通道的精确处理）对电子固定靶实验中轻暗物质信号预测至关重要，可显著修正信号率估计，建议在高精度实验分析中采用此类方法替代简单唯象参数化。

Abstract: Accelerator-based experiments at the intensity frontier, in which a high-energy beam impinges on a nuclear target, serve as powerful probes of the light dark matter paradigm. Such experiments require precise modeling of the target nucleus for reliable signal predictions. We present the application of a many-body ab initio method to calculate light dark matter mediator production signal rates at electron fixed-target experiments, using chiral effective field theory interactions. Considering both elastic and quasi elastic scattering, we compute cross sections using a Monte Carlo event generator implementation informed by ab initio nuclear elastic form factors and spectral functions for three representative nuclei, $^{20}$Ne, $^{34}$Si, and $^{56}$Fe, at varying electron beam energies. We compare our results to a commonly used phenomenological parameterization, finding an increased signal yield by up to two orders of magnitude with our quasi elastic treatment and an agreement for lighter mediators.

</details>


### [145] [Getting Warmer: IceCube Nears Freeze Out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26258)
*Mainak Mukhopadhyay,Gordan Krnjaic*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用IceCube探测到的银河系弥散高能中微子信号，通过两种互补方法对暗物质湮灭为中微子的截面设置新限制，并展示未来升级探测器将大幅提升此灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰立方（IceCube）近期探测到来自银河系的高能中微子弥散通量，为利用这些观测信号约束暗物质性质提供了新的机会。此前对暗物质湮灭为中微子的截面限制较弱，需要新的方法来获得更强且更可靠的约束。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：（1）基于扣除背景后的银河系经度分布的簇射事件，放置了一个不依赖于任何银河系宇宙线发射模型的保守暗物质湮灭截面上限；（2）基于不同宇宙线发射模型下推断的内星系中微子能量谱（模板依赖方法），检验这些谱对暗物质贡献的敏感性。

Result: 第一种方法得到的湮灭截面限制比现有约束改善了数倍；第二种方法表明推断的银河系中微子强度已对接近热遗迹基准的暗物质贡献敏感（对TeV尺度暗物质质量范围），但更依赖模型假设。

Conclusion: 银河系弥散中微子发射的测量可作为探测暗物质湮灭为中微子的强大工具。未来的IceCube-Gen2和KM3NeT观测将大幅延伸此灵敏度，有望对热冻结机制进行决定性检验。

Abstract: IceCube has recently detected a diffuse population of high-energy neutrinos arising from the Milky Way. We use this high-significance detection to place new limits on dark matter (DM) annihilation to neutrinos with two complementary approaches. The first method uses the background-subtracted Galactic longitude distribution of shower events to place a conservative bound on the DM annihilation cross section that does not rely on any assumed Galactic cosmic ray emission model; the resulting limits on the velocity-averaged annihilation cross section improve upon existing bounds by factors of a few. The second method uses the template-dependent neutrino energy spectra from the Inner Galaxy, inferred under different Galactic cosmic ray emission models. This complementary approach shows that the inferred Galactic neutrino intensities are already sensitive to DM contributions near the thermal-relic benchmark for a range of TeV-scale DM masses, though this comparison is more model-dependent. Our results demonstrate that measurements of diffuse Galactic neutrino emission can be used as a powerful probe of DM annihilation into neutrinos. Future observations with IceCube-Gen2 and KM3NeT will substantially extend this sensitivity, potentially allowing a decisive test of the thermal freeze-out mechanism with Galactic neutrino observations.

</details>


### [146] [Enhancement of nonlinear pair production in a flying-focus pulse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26313)
*Md Reshad Ur Rahman,Martin S. Formanek,Elias Gerstmayr,Dillon Ramsey,John P. Palastro,Antonino Di Piazza*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究表明，飞行聚焦（FF）脉冲相比传统驻点高斯（SFG）激光脉冲，能更有效地增强非线性Breit-Wheeler对产生（NBWPP）的产额。当FF脉冲的焦点以光速与高能光子束共向传播、而脉冲本身与光子束反向传播时，对10、20和50 GeV的光子，对产额分别提升12%、29%和76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高强度激光场中，高能伽马光子可衰变为电子-正电子对（非线性Breit-Wheeler过程）。在足够强的脉冲下，NBWPP概率随相互作用时间的变化比随场强变化更有利，因此延长光子与强场区域的相互作用时间可显著提高对产生效率。传统驻点高斯脉冲的焦点静止，限制了有效相互作用时间；而飞行聚焦脉冲的焦点可以可编程速度移动，有望通过优化焦点运动来增强对产生产额。

Method: 作者提出采用飞行聚焦（FF）脉冲，使其焦点以光速与高能光子束同向传播，而脉冲本身与光子束反向传播，从而延长光子与强激光场的有效相互作用时间。通过数值模拟，将焦耳级紧聚焦FF脉冲与等能量SFG脉冲的对产生产额进行比较，针对不同能量（10、20、50 GeV）的入射光子进行评估。

Result: 数值模拟表明，对于10、20和50 GeV的光子，焦耳级紧聚焦FF脉冲相比等能量SFG脉冲，对产生产额分别提升了12%、29%和76%，且提升幅度随光子能量增加而增大。

Conclusion: 在所述参数区间内，飞行聚焦脉冲比传统驻点高斯脉冲更高效地产生电子-正电子对，为高强度激光驱动的非线性量子电动力学过程提供了一种新的增强手段。

Abstract: A photon can decay into an electron-positron pair in the presence of an intense laser pulse by a process known as nonlinear Breit-Wheeler pair production (NBWPP). For a sufficiently intense pulse, the probability of NBWPP for a high-energy gamma photon scales more favorably with the interaction time than with the field intensity. In contrast to typical stationary-focus Gaussian (SFG) laser pulses, a flying-focus (FF) pulse features a focal point that moves at a programmable velocity. Here, we show that a FF pulse counterpropagating with a high-energy photon beam while its focus copropagates with the beam at the speed of light enhances the NBWPP yield relative to an equal-energy SFG pulse. Numerical simulations show that a Joule-class tightly-focused FF pulse allows for an enhancement of the pair-production yield by 12\%, 29\% and 76\% for a photon of 10, 20 and 50 GeV energy, respectively. Thus, in this regime, FF pulses are more efficient at producing electron-positron pairs than conventional SFG pulses.

</details>


### [147] [Direct and Indirect searches for DM-electron interactions in sub-GeV DM models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26467)
*Marco Cirelli,Arpan Kar,Antoine Letessier-Selvon,Harrys Lumengo-Kidimbu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了亚GeV暗物质直接探测（DD）与间接探测（ID）之间的互补性，聚焦于矢量门户（暗光子）和标量门户（希格斯门户）两种现实模型，发现矢量门户模型中DAMIC-M与ID的互补性最佳，而ID总体上约束更强，甚至可触及中微子地板以下的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚GeV暗物质的直接探测与间接探测手段各有优劣，但目前缺乏在现实模型框架下系统对比两者互补性的研究。理解DD与ID的联合约束能力，对于设计未来实验策略和缩小暗物质参数空间至关重要。

Method: 以暗光子（矢量门户）和希格斯门户（标量门户）两种现实模型为框架；DD方面采用DAMIC-M当前及未来投影对暗物质-电子散射的限制；ID方面考虑现有X射线/伽马射线和宇宙射线正负电子观测以及未来MeV望远镜COSI，获得湮灭率约束后转化为电子散射截面并与DD对比；同时纳入天体物理和银河系传播的不确定性。

Result: 矢量门户模型中DAMIC-M与ID互补性最佳，尤其在矢量媒介子重于暗物质情况下，当前DAMIC-M约束在3-20 MeV质量区间可比拟甚至优于综合ID限制；其他情形下ID总体约束更强，部分质量区间可探测到中微子地板以下的参数空间。

Conclusion: DD与ID在亚GeV暗物质搜索中具有显著的互补性，其相对效力取决于具体模型和媒介子-暗物质质量关系；ID在多数情形下更具约束力，而DAMIC-M在特定模型参数下可与ID相当，为未来暗物质探测实验设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We analyze the complementarity between direct detection (DD) and indirect detection (ID) searches of sub-GeV dark matter (DM) in the context of two realistic models: the vector-portal (dark photon) and scalar-portal (higgs-portal) models. For DD we focus on the latest constraints on the DM-electron scattering from the present DAMIC-M experiment as well as its future projection. For ID we consider several existing X-rays/gamma-rays and cosmic-ray ($e^\pm$) observations as well as the upcoming MeV telescope COSI to obtain the corresponding bounds and projections on the DM annihilation rate, which are then translated in terms of the DM-electron scattering cross-section and compared with the DD bounds/projections. We properly take into account astrophysical and galactic propagation uncertainties. We find that the complementarity between DAMIC-M and ID works best for the vector-portal model, especially for the case where the vector mediator is heavier than the DM particle: in this case, the present DAMIC-M bound becomes comparable to (or better than, for some astrophysical configurations) the combined ID limit for $3\,{\rm MeV} \lesssim m_{\rm DM} \lesssim 20\,{\rm MeV}$. For other cases and mass ranges, the ID is in general more constraining and can also, for some mass ranges, constrain the parameter space below the neutrino floor, which is inaccessible to DD experiments.

</details>


### [148] [Atomic structure calculations for constraining new electron-electron forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26532)
*Cameron J. West,Benjamin M. Roberts*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种利用原子结构理论计算新电子-电子相互作用的通用方法，包含主要多体效应，并为宇称破缺情形提取耦合强度界限。通过含时Hartree-Fock方法纳入新相互作用的多体修正，对标量-赝标量和矢量-轴矢量相互作用分别结合原子电偶极矩和宇称不守恒（PNC）跃迁实验提取耦合上限。矢量-轴矢量情形下排除了m_X ~ 10 MeV以上区域内|g^e_V g^e_A|/m_X^2 > 10^-11 MeV^-2的耦合质量比，覆盖了此前未受约束的质量区域，并更新了铯6s-7s PNC跃迁振幅E_PNC中标准模型电子-电子Z^0交换贡献的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在建立一种通用的原子尺度计算方法，用于探测超越标准模型的新电子-电子相互作用（如轻暗物质或新规范玻色子介导），并通过精确纳入多体效应来改进此前仅依赖单粒子近似的计算结果，从而对更广泛的介子质量范围给出更可靠的实验约束。

Method: 采用原子结构理论框架，利用含时Hartree-Fock（TDHF）方法系统纳入新相互作用引起的多体关联修正；该方法对任意介子质量均有效。计算了标量-赝标量（s-p）相互作用对原子电偶极矩（EDM）的贡献，以及矢量-轴矢量（v-a）相互作用对PNC跃迁振幅的贡献，并与实验测量比较提取耦合上限。同时更新了标准模型Z^0玻色子交换对铯6s-7s PNC跃迁振幅E_PNC的贡献计算。

Result: 对于矢量-轴矢量相互作用，在介子质量m_X ~ 10 MeV以上区域排除了|g^e_V g^e_A|/m_X^2大于10^-11 MeV^-2的所有耦合情况，填补了此前实验未覆盖的质量范围。通过多体修正显著提高了计算的可靠性，给出更严格的耦合界限，并提供更新后的铯PNC跃迁振幅中Z^0交换贡献数值。

Conclusion: 该工作表明含时Hartree-Fock方法能够高效且可靠地纳入新相互作用的原子多体效应，为任意介子质量的新物理搜索提供了统一的原子尺度探针，并成功覆盖了此前未受约束的质量区域，对超出标准模型的新轻粒子搜索具有重要价值。

Abstract: We present a general approach for calculating new electron-electron interactions with atomic structure theory, include dominant many-body effects, and extract bounds for parity-odd cases. The procedure is valid for any mediator mass and improves previous calculations by including many-body corrections to the new interaction through the time-dependent Hartree-Fock method. Bounds on coupling strengths are then extracted for scalar-pseudoscalar and vector-axial vector interactions by comparison with experimental atomic electric dipole moments and parity non-conserving (PNC) transitions respectively. In the latter case, our calculations rule out any coupling-mass ratio |g^e_V g^e_A|/m_X^2 greater than 10^-11 MeV^-2 above m_X ~ 10 MeV, covering a previously unconstrained mass region. We also report an updated calculation of the regular (Standard Model) electron-electron Z^0 exchange contribution to the 6s-7s PNC transition amplitude E_PNC in cesium.

</details>


### [149] [Fermi gas of domain-wall Skyrmions in QCD in a strong magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26608)
*Patrick Copinger,Minoru Eto,Muneto Nitta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了手征孤子晶格中畴壁Skyrmion的电磁屏蔽特性，发现其密度在零温下饱和至费米动量，并预测了有限温度下的Debye屏蔽效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 畴壁Skyrmion作为双味手征微扰论在磁场中的基态，与量子色动力学（QCD）中的重子相关，理解其电磁屏蔽特性有助于揭示磁场和化学势相图上的重子密度分布。

Method: 采用模量有效理论分析零温下Skyrmion密度饱和至费米动量的行为；将手征微扰论与量子电动力学（QED）耦合，通过联合Debye质量/长度推导有限温度下的静态费米子畴壁Skyrmion屏蔽。

Result: 零温下发现Skyrmion密度在垂直于磁场的手征孤子晶格圆盘上饱和至费米动量；有限温度下预测了静态费米子畴壁Skyrmion的屏蔽现象，由联合Debye质量/长度描述。

Conclusion: 研究为畴壁Skyrmion的电磁屏蔽行为提供了理论框架，建立了与重子密度在磁场-化学势相图上的联系，并给出了有限温度的屏蔽预测。

Abstract: Fermionic domain-wall Skyrmions arise in the chiral soliton lattice of two flavor chiral perturbation theory in a magnetic field as the ground state and are associated with baryons of the underlying theory of quantum chromodynamics. We analyze the electromagnetic screening characteristics of domain-wall Skyrmions that give insight into baryon density on the magnetic field and chemical potential phase diagram. At zero temperature, using the moduli effective theory, it is found Skyrmion density is saturated up to the Fermi momenta on the disk of the chiral soliton lattice orthogonal to the magnetic field. We further study chiral perturbation theory coupled to quantum electrodynamics leading to finite temperature screening, wherein a static fermionic domain-wall Skyrmion screening is predicted through a combined Debye mass/length.

</details>


### [150] [Post-sphaleron conversion between baryon and lepton numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26670)
*Pei-Hong Gu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现，在合适的标量粒子和其他TeV尺度新物理存在下，重子-轻子转换在电弱sphaleron过程失效后仍能保持高效，该方案可同时解释中子寿命之谜，并预言多核子衰变到多轻子的新衰变模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重子数与轻子数之间的转换对宇宙中物质-反物质不对称性的动态产生机制至关重要。传统观点认为这种转换来源于电弱sphaleron过程，但需要探索在电弱sphaleron不再活跃后是否存在其他有效途径。

Method: 作者提出在电弱sphaleron过程失效后，通过引入合适的标量粒子与其他TeV尺度新物理的协同作用，使重子-轻子转换仍然保持高效。

Result: 该方案可为中子寿命之谜提供解决方案，并预言了多核子衰变为多轻子的若干新衰变模式。

Conclusion: 重子-轻子转换机制可以超越传统电弱sphaleron框架而继续有效，为宇宙物质-反物质不对称性和中子寿命问题提供统一的新物理解释。

Abstract: The conversion between baryon and lepton numbers has a significant impact on the mechanisms for dynamically generating the matter-antimatter asymmetry in the present universe. Under the traditional wisdom, such conversion originates from the so-called electroweak sphaleron processes. In this work we find that in association with a proper scalar and the other TeV new physics, the baryon-lepton conversion can still keep efficient after the electroweak sphalerons are no longer active. Moreover this scenario can provide a solution to the puzzle of neutron lifetime and predict some decay modes of multi-nucleons to multi-leptons.

</details>


### [151] [From Thermal History to Multi-Messenger Signatures in $\mathbb{Z}_3$ Symmetric Dark Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26682)
*Debajyoti Choudhury,Jaydeb Das,Divya Sachdeva*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个基于Z₃对称性的标准模型扩展框架，包含右手中微子、暗费米子和暗复标量，通过湮灭、半湮灭和暗区转换过程实现暗物质 relic 密度，并产生可被未来空间引力波探测器（LISA、BBO、DECIGO）探测的引力波信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种能够同时解释暗物质存在、中微子质量起源以及电弱相变一阶性质的统一理论框架，并评估其多信使观测信号的可探测性。

Method: 构建Z₃对称的标准模型扩展模型，引入右手中微子N_R、暗费米子χ（暗物质候选体）和暗复标量S。通过湮灭、半湮灭和暗区转换过程计算热 relic 密度，同时要求满足强一阶电弱相变（SFOEWPT）条件；随后计算环诱导的直接探测、γ射线间接探测和引力波信号的预言。

Result: 在满足SFOEWPT的广泛参数空间区域中，观测到的relic密度可通过多种暗区过程的联合效应实现；引力波信号强度落在LISA、BBO和DECIGO等未来空间探测器灵敏度范围内。

Conclusion: 该Z₃对称模型为暗物质与强一阶电弱相变提供了自洽的解释，且其预言的多信使信号具有在未来实验中被检验的潜力，尤其以引力波探测最具前景。

Abstract: We investigate a $\mathbb{Z}_3$-symmetric extension of the Standard Model consisting of a right-handed neutrino ($N_R$), a dark fermion ($χ$) that dominates the relic density and a dark complex scalar ($S$) that facilitates a strong first-order electroweak phase transition (SFOEWPT). The observed relic abundance, is achieved through the combined effects of annihilation, semi-annihilation, and dark-sector conversion processes over a broad region of parameter space consistent with a SFOEWPT. We further investigate the resulting multi-messenger signatures, including loop-induced direct detection, indirect detection through gamma-ray observations, and gravitational wave signals, with the latter lying within the projected sensitivities of future space-based detectors such as LISA, BBO, and DECIGO.

</details>


### [152] [$μ$DM: a new mechanism for the baryon-dark matter coincidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26709)
*Wen Yin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一类名为"化学势匹配暗物质"(μDM)的新模型，通过使重子数化学势|μ_B|与暗物质质量m_DM量级相当（|μ_B|~m_DM），从而建立暗物质与重子丰度巧合的新机制。以Affleck-Dine场作为暗物质的具体示例，并讨论了与轴子类粒子壁相关的自发电弱重子生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙中暗物质与重子物质能量密度处于同一数量级（巧合问题），现有模型通常需要精细调节参数来解释这一巧合。作者希望提出一种新的理论路径，从化学势匹配的角度自然统一暗物质丰度与重子不对称性的起源。

Method: 作者观察到，相对论退耦热 relics的暗物质-熵比满足ρ_DM/s~10^-3 m_DM，而弱子圈冻结后重子不对称性可参数化为ρ_baryon/s~10^-4|μ_B|。通过设定|μ_B|~m_DM，两个丰度自然匹配。具体实现采用Affleck-Dine场作为暗物质载体，并讨论无熵稀释情况下热产生由玻色增强受激发射主导以获得冷动量分布。

Result: 在无熵稀释的情况下，该巧合机制通常指向eV-keV量级的暗物质质量；若采用玻色增强受激发射主导的热产生，动量分布可以保持冷酷。若在暗物质和重子产额确定后进行熵稀释，则可允许质量高达MeV量级。

Conclusion: 化学势匹配提供了一条新的解决重子-暗物质巧合问题的方法，扩展了暗物质质量允许范围，并可与Affleck-Dine机制及自发电弱重子生成等物理场景自然结合。

Abstract: For a relativistically decoupled thermal relic, the dark-matter energy-to-entropy ratio scales as $ρ_{\rm DM}/s\sim 10^{-3}m_{\rm DM}$. This is the familiar hot-dark-matter abundance relation. Immediately after weak-sphaleron freeze-out, the baryon asymmetry can be parametrized as $ρ_{\rm baryon}/s\sim 10^{-4}|μ_B|$. Here $μ_B$ is the baryon-number chemical potential. We propose a new class of models, called chemical-potential-matched dark matter ($μ$DM), in which $|μ_B|\sim m_{\rm DM}$, thereby providing a new route to the baryon-dark matter coincidence. As a concrete example of $μ$DM, we consider an Affleck-Dine field whose excitations constitute dark matter. We also briefly discuss spontaneous electroweak baryogenesis associated with axion-like-particle walls. In the absence of entropy dilution, this coincidence mechanism generically points to dark matter in the eV-keV mass range. Its momentum distribution can nevertheless be cold if thermal production is dominated by Bose-enhanced stimulated emission. Alternatively, entropy dilution after both the dark-matter and baryon yields are fixed allows masses up to the MeV scale.

</details>


### [153] [Simulations and flavour-scheme studies for Higgs-boson production in association with charm quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26863)
*Tiziano Bevilacqua,Christian Biello,Lea Michela Caminada,Clemens Lange,Marino Missiroli,Davide Pagani,Michele Selvaggi,Marco Zaro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对希格斯玻色子与粲夸克和底夸克联合产生的过程进行了详细的NLO+PS研究，重点聚焦H+c末态，系统比较了有质量与无质量味方案，量化了标度和味方案不确定性，并对粲夸克汤川聚变产生希格斯的通道给出了首个实用的LHC模拟建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H+c过程是研究粲夸克汤川耦合的重要通道，但不同味方案（4FS vs 5FS）和理论框架下预测差异显著，缺乏系统的比较和模拟指导，无法为LHC实验分析提供可靠的理论输入。

Method: 在有质量（4FS）和无质量（5FS）味方案中系统比较NLO+PS预测；量化标度不确定性和味方案不确定性；评估干涉效应和圈诱导贡献；将粧夸克汤川聚变H+c的NLO+PS预测与4FS下ccbarH产生的NNLO+PS计算结果在13.6 TeV下进行对比。

Result: 给出了H+c产生在不同味方案下的系统比较结果，识别了理论不确定性的主要来源，并在13.6 TeV下将c cbar H产生的NLO+PS与NNLO+PS预测进行了对比。

Conclusion: 基于系统性比较的结果，首次为LHC分析中ccbarH产生的模拟提供了实用的推荐方案。

Abstract: We present a detailed NLO+PS study of Higgs-boson production in association with charm and bottom quarks, with particular focus on the modelling of the H+c final state. We systematically compare predictions in massive and massless flavour schemes, quantify scale and flavour-scheme uncertainties, and assess the impact of interference and loop-induced contributions. Special emphasis is placed on the Higgs production via charm Yukawa fusion, for which we compare NLO+PS predictions with a NNLO+PS calculation of $c\bar{c}H$ production in the four-flavour scheme at 13.6 TeV. Based on these results, we provide the first practical recommendations for the simulation of $c\bar{c}H$ production in LHC analyses.

</details>


### [154] [Advances in the holographic description of hot and dense QCD matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27023)
*Nicolas Kovensky,Andreas Schmitt*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文回顾并扩展了规范-引力对偶框架下QCD类物质在非零温度和化学势下的相结构研究，聚焦于Witten-Sakai-Sugimoto模型的顶-底(string theory)方法，该方法可统一处理介子、重子和夸克。文中首次构建了介于BEC与BCS区之间的pion凝聚相的全息实现(虽为亚稳态)，并讨论了该方法的预言、局限及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非零温度和化学势下，QCD存在多个丰富的相(如pion凝聚、重子物质、夸克物质等)，但第一性原理(S如格点QCD)在处理有限密度系统时面临符号问题等困难。规范-引力对偶为研究这些区域提供了互补的理论工具，本文旨在系统回顾和推进这一方向。

Method: 基于Witten-Sakai-Sugimoto全息模型(一种弦理论顶-底方法)，构建不同QCD相(包括pion凝聚、重子态、夸克态)的引力对偶构型，通过计算热力学势来确定相图结构，并首次尝试全息构造介于BEC与BCS之间的pion凝聚相。

Result: 系统总结了该全息模型下QCD相图的主要特征与预言，识别了模型的弱点与限制；创新性地给出了BEC-BCS过渡区间的pion凝聚相的全息构造，但发现该相在热力学上是亚稳态的。

Conclusion: 全息QCD(特别是Witten-Sakai-Sugimoto模型)为探索有限密度QCD相结构提供了有价值的工具，但存在固有局限性，且新构造的BEC-BCS区间pion相为亚稳态，提示需要进一步改进模型或探索更广泛的构型空间，并为未来研究方向指明了途径。

Abstract: We review and extend recent progress on QCD-like phases at nonzero temperature and chemical potentials within the gauge-gravity duality. We focus on the Witten-Sakai-Sugimoto model, a top-down approach based on string theory which allows for a unified treatment of mesons, baryons and quarks. Phases such as pion condensation, baryonic and quarkyonic matter are located in the phase diagram, and we discuss predictions as well as weaknesses and limitations of this holographic approach. One of the novelties included in this review is the first holographic construction of a pionic phase between BEC and BCS regimes, which however is found to be metastable. We also discuss future directions in holographic QCD, within the model used here and in a wider context.

</details>


### [155] [Learning Transverse Momentum Distributions from Raw Scattering Events via Conditional Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27077)
*Jitao Xu,Christopher Cocuzza,Kevin Braga,Daniel Lersch,Nobuo Sato,Yaohang Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种条件扩散模型，直接从SIDIS事件运动学映射到横动量依赖部分子分布函数（TMD PDFs），绕过了传统参数化函数形式和迭代拟合的限制。在CLAS12运动学模拟数据上，该模型能够恢复底层TMD，提供随事件统计量增加而逐渐收紧的不确定性估计，即使在仅1000个条件事件的统计受限条件下也能产生可靠估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从SIDIS数据中提取TMD PDFs是美国Jefferson Lab和未来电子-离子对撞机（EIC）核子结构计划的核心目标。传统提取方法依赖参数化函数形式和迭代拟合，这限制了所得分布的灵活性，并使不确定性量化变得繁琐。因此需要一种更灵活、能直接学习映射关系且天然支持不确定性估计的新方法。

Method: 提出了一种条件扩散模型（conditional diffusion model），它学习从原始SIDIS事件运动学直接映射到TMD PDFs，完全绕过对分布函数的显式功能形式假设。模型条件化在事件运动学上生成TMD分布，不需要预先设定参数化形式。

Result: 在CLAS12运动学下的模拟SIDIS数据上进行评估，模型成功恢复了底层TMD分布；产生的不确定性信息丰富且随事件统计量增加而稳定收窄；即使在仅1000个条件事件的统计受限条件下（与当前和计划实验直接相关的低统计量区间），仍能产生可靠的估计。

Conclusion: 条件扩散模型为TMD PDF提取提供了一种无需显式参数化假设的新范式，能够在统计受限的实验条件下产生可靠性高且不确定性可量化（且随统计量收敛）的TMD提取结果，对Jefferson Lab和EIC的实验分析具有直接应用价值。

Abstract: Extracting transverse momentum dependent parton distribution functions (TMD PDFs) from semi-inclusive deep inelastic scattering (SIDIS) data is a central goal of the nucleon structure program at Jefferson Lab and the future Electron-Ion Collider. Traditional extraction methods rely on parameterized functional forms and iterative fitting, which can limit the flexibility of the resulting distributions and make uncertainty quantification cumbersome. We present a conditional diffusion model that learns to map raw SIDIS event kinematics directly to TMD PDFs, bypassing explicit functional assumptions. Evaluated on simulated SIDIS data at CLAS12 kinematics, the model recovers the underlying TMD with informative uncertainties that narrow steadily with increasing event statistics, and produces reliable estimates even with as few as 1,000 conditioning events, a statistics-limited regime directly relevant to ongoing and planned experiments.

</details>


### [156] [Too good to go: Upcycling Phase-Space Points for Multijet Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27104)
*Konrad Helms,Timo Janßen,Steffen Schumann*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用相空间嵌套结构的训练策略，通过将N粒子相空间的采样器增强为(N+1)粒子数据集，显著降低高维相空间采样器的训练成本并提升性能，适用于机器学习采样器（如连续归一化流）和传统算法（如VEGAS），在LHC的Drell-Yan和top-pair产生过程中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙特卡洛事件生成器在高维相空间中的高效采样面临重大挑战，尤其在高多重数末态下矩阵元评估计算成本高昂，现有采样器的适应（训练）过程代价大。

Method: 利用相空间的嵌套结构——(N+1)粒子相空间可分解为N粒子相空间与单粒子相空间的乘积。假设已有高效的N粒子采样器（如QCD X+n-jet过程堆叠），将其增强为(N+1)粒子数据集，所得训练样本比均匀采样更接近被积函数且统计量更大。该增强样本仅用作初始提议分布，通过精确事件加权保证无偏的蒙特卡洛估计，方法对采样器类型无关（适用于ML方法和VEGAS等传统算法）。

Result: 在LHC的类Drell-Yan和top-pair产生过程中，使用连续归一化流采样器演示了矩阵元评估次数的减少以及最终性能的提升。

Conclusion: 该方法能显著降低高维相空间采样器适应阶段的成本，同时提供优于现有基准的采样器，且不依赖特定采样算法，通用性强。

Abstract: The efficient sampling of high-dimensional phase spaces is a major challenge for Monte Carlo event generators, as for high-multiplicity final states the evaluation of scattering matrix elements becomes computationally expensive. We here introduce a training strategy that significantly reduces the cost of adapting the samplers, while delivering samplers that outperform the current benchmarks. The method exploits the nested structure of phase spaces, where an $(N+1)$-particle phase space factorises into an $N$-particle and a one-particle phase space. We thereby assume that an efficient sampler for the corresponding $N$-particle phase space is already available, as is the case in stacks of QCD $X+n$-jets processes. By augmenting an $N$-particle to an $(N+1)$-particle dataset, we obtain a training sample that more closely resembles the integrand and is statistically larger than, for example, a uniformly sampled one. Starting the adaptation phase of the sampler with a well-sampled $N$-particle core accelerates learning of the full $(N+1)$-particle phase-space density. Since the augmented sample is only used as an initial proposal distribution, unbiased Monte Carlo estimates are still guaranteed by exact event weighting. The approach is agnostic to the trained sampler and can be applied to machine-learning-based methods as well as more traditional algorithms such as VEGAS. We demonstrate the reduction in matrix-element evaluations and final performance increase for jet-associated Drell--Yan and top-pair production at the LHC, using Continuous Normalising Flow samplers.

</details>


### [157] [Exploring $Z/γ$-mediated heavy FCNCs at the FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27183)
*Abhik Sarkar,Subhajit Kala,Amir Subba,Yu Shi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究SMEFT框架下第三代与第一、二代之间由Z/γ中性规范玻色子介导的味破缺跃迁，利用最优观测量技术评估未来FCC-ee对撞机在不同质心能量下的探测灵敏度，并与低能味物理观测量和重费米子衰变通道的约束进行互补分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型中第三代费米子的质量显著大于前两代且性质测量精度较低，使其成为寻找超出标准模型新物理的关键窗口。第三代与轻代之间的味跃迁为探索新物理提供了独特视角，但当前缺乏对FCC-ee在不同能量阶段系统探测这种味破缺相互作用的全面评估。

Method: 在SMEFT框架下，使用偶极算符和Higgs-流算符描述第三代与前两代之间的Z/γ介导味破缺跃迁；采用最优观测量技术（OOT）计算FCC-ee在不同质心能量下的最优灵敏度；同时从低能味破缺观测量和重费米子衰变通道推导相应的互补约束，并分析偶极贡献之间的干涉模式。

Result: FCC-ee在电弱能标提供对味破缺相互作用的直接且互补的探测能力：部分相互作用的灵敏度投影显著改善，另一些与现有低能味约束相当。分析还揭示了偶极贡献中依赖于具体味跃迁的干涉模式，且该模式在Z极点与高能FCC-ee阶段之间表现出不同行为。

Conclusion: FCC-ee各能量阶段的系统评估对全面理解其探索味破缺现象的潜力至关重要，其与低能味实验的互补性凸显了跨能标协同搜索新物理的必要性。

Abstract: The flavor structure of the Standard Model (SM) remains one of the most compelling questions in particle physics, with the third generation being particularly intriguing due to its significantly larger masses and comparatively less precisely measured properties. These features make third-generation flavor transitions particularly interesting in context of search for physics beyond the SM. In this work, we investigate flavor-violating transitions between the third and the first two generations, mediated by the neutral gauge bosons ($Z/γ$), within the framework of the SM Effective Field Theory (SMEFT), using dipole and Higgs-current operators. We determine the optimal sensitivities using the optimal observable technique (OOT) at different center-of-mass energies of the upcoming Future Circular Collider in the $e^+e^-$ mode (FCC-ee). We further derive complementary constraints on the relevant SMEFT operators from low-energy flavor-violating observables and heavy fermion decay channels. Our analysis also reveals characteristic interference patterns among the dipole contributions, which depend on the underlying flavor transition and exhibit distinct behavior between the $Z$ pole and higher-energy FCC-ee stages. The FCC-ee provides a complementary and direct probe of flavor-violating interactions at the electroweak scale, with the projections showing improved sensitivity for several interactions and comparable sensitivity to existing flavor constraints for several others. This highlights the importance of a systematic assessment across the different FCC-ee energy stages, which provides a comprehensive picture of its potential to explore flavor-violating phenomena and its complementarity with low-energy flavor experiments.

</details>


### [158] [Enigmatic properties of $Ξ(1620)$ and $Ξ(1690)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27236)
*Taísa Veloso,K. P. Khemchandani,A. Martinez Torres,Luciano M. Abreu,Li-Sheng Geng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用耦合道方法研究粲数为零的双奇异超子Ξ(1620)和Ξ(1690)的介子-重子相互作用，尝试同时描述K⁻Λ飞米尺度关联函数数据和πΞ、K̄Σ、K̄Λ等不变质量分布数据，但发现两种数据无法被同一振幅同时满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来多个实验合作组发布了有关双奇异超子Ξ(1620)和Ξ(1690)前两个激发态的大量数据，需要从理论上对这些数据进行统一的物理解释。

Method: 在耦合道框架下研究由赝标介子和矢量介子组成的介子-重子系统，在总自旋1/2和同位旋1/2的组态下，基于手征对称性和隐藏局域对称性的拉格朗日量推导树级相互作用，并进行计算。

Result: 研究发现，能够给出与实验数据接近的K⁻Λ飞米尺度关联函数的振幅，其预言的不同介子-重子系统质量分布与实验数据不一致；反之亦然，即两组数据无法同时被同一振幅所描述。

Conclusion: K⁻Λ飞米尺度关联函数数据与πΞ、K̄Σ和K̄Λ不变质量分布数据之间存在张力，目前的耦合道模型无法同时自洽地解释这两类实验结果。

Abstract: Motivated by a considerable release of data on first two excited states of the doubly strange baryon, $Ξ(1620)$ and $Ξ(1690)$, from different experimental collaborations in recent years, we investigate meson-baryon interactions in a coupled-channel approach. The systems are composed of pseudoscalar and vector mesons and the calculations are done for the total spin 1/2 and isospin 1/2 configuration. The tree-level interactions are deduced from Lagrangians based on chiral and hidden local symmetries. Specifically, we make an attempt to describe (1) data on the $K^-Λ$ femtoscopic correlation function coming from $p\,p$ and $Pb\,Pb$ collisions, and (2) the available data on invariant mass distributions for different systems: $πΞ$, $\bar K Σ$ and $\bar K Λ$. We find that the amplitude which yields the correlation function close to the data results in mass distributions of different meson-baryon systems that are in discordance with the experimental data and vice-versa.

</details>


### [159] [Hunting long-lived doubly charged scalars at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27251)
*Biplob Bhattacherjee,Rituparna Ghosh,Swagata Mukherjee,Nabin Kumar Pidikaka*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了长寿命双电荷标量粒子的对撞机现象学，发现了传统搜索在cτ约为0.1-100毫米范围内灵敏度缺失的中间区域，并提出了在HL-LHC上利用（位移顶点）搜索来探测质量高达约1 TeV的双电荷标量，通过重建不变量质量切割可显著压制标准模型背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对双电荷标量的搜索要么针对瞬时衰变（prompt）场景，要么针对极长寿命（稳定带电粒子）场景，但在cτ约数百微米到数厘米的中间寿命区间内传统搜索失去灵敏度，这一未被覆盖的参数空间需要新的搜索策略。

Method: 作者分析了双电荷标量在存在和不存在ΔL=2汤川耦合两种情况下的寿命特性；提出了HL-LHC上位移顶点搜索方案，利用从关联径迹重建的位移顶点不变量质量进行切割以压制标准模型背景；给出了√s=14 TeV、积分亮度3000 fb⁻¹下Drell-Yan对产生截面的预期排除极限。

Result: 在存在ΔL=2耦合时，双电荷标量仅能在100-150 GeV质量范围内是长寿命的；在无费米子耦合（fermiophobic）场景下，该范围扩展到TeV尺度。提出的位移顶点搜索可在fermiophobic场景下探测质量高达约1 TeV、cτ=10-100 mm的双电荷标量；在耦合到轻子时考虑120 GeV、cτ=5 mm的基准点。不变量质量切割能有效压制SM背景。

Conclusion: 位移顶点搜索为传统瞬时衰变搜索和重稳定带电粒子搜索所覆盖的区域提供了一个互补的探测空间，是覆盖中间寿命区间双电荷标量参数空间的有效手段。

Abstract: This work studies the collider phenomenology of long-lived doubly charged scalars. After reviewing the existing searches for the doubly charged scalar in both the prompt and the long-lived regimes, we identify an intermediate range of proper decay length, namely, $\mathcal{O}(0.1~\mathrm{mm}) \lesssim cτ\lesssim \mathcal{O}(100~\mathrm{mm})$, where conventional searches lose sensitivity. We investigate the prospects for probing the doubly charged scalar both in the presence and in the absence of the $ΔL=2$ Yukawa coupling of the $SU(2)_{L}$ complex triplet. In the presence of this coupling, $H^{\pm\pm}$ can be long-lived only for masses in the range $100$-$150~\mathrm{GeV}$, whereas in the fermiophobic scenario (i.e., in the absence of the $ΔL=2$ coupling), the range extends to $\mathrm{TeV}$ scale. We propose a displaced-vertex search at the HL-LHC for doubly charged scalars with masses up to $\approx 1~\mathrm{TeV}$ and $cτ=10$-$100~\mathrm{mm}$ in the fermiophobic scenario, while a benchmark point with a doubly charged scalar of mass $120~\mathrm{GeV}$ and $cτ= 5~\mathrm{mm}$ is considered when the complex triplet scalar couples to leptons. We show that a cut on the invariant mass of the displaced vertex as reconstructed from the associated tracks can strongly suppress Standard Model backgrounds. We present projected limits on the Drell-Yan pair-production cross section for the doubly charged scalar at $\sqrt{s}=14~\mathrm{TeV}$ with an integrated luminosity of $3000~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$, considering two illustrative assumptions for the residual background. Displaced-vertex searches thus probe a region complementary to those covered by prompt and heavy stable charged-particle searches.

</details>


### [160] [An Axial $U_A(1)_{L_μ-L_τ}$: UV Completion and Experimental Searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27258)
*Rundong Fang,Jinhui Guo,Ming Li,Jia Liu,Xiao-Ping Wang,Yiheng Xiong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个无反常、可重整化的轴矢量 U_A(1)_{L_μ-L_τ} 模型，通过 universal-seesaw 机制产生带电轻子质量，并系统研究了该模型在 MeV 到 TeV 能标下 A' 玻色子的实验特征。研究发现，与通常的 L_μ-L_τ 模型不同，A' 修正的是轴矢量弱耦合系数而非矢量系数；纵模会增强 muon 轫致辐射并对 (g-2)_μ 产生负贡献。通过与中微子三叉产生、不可见介子衰变、四缪子共振搜索等现有实验数据对比，作者在 (m_{A'}, g_X) 平面上给出了唯象学约束，并提出未来缪子对撞机上的角分布（特别是前后不对称性）可有效分辨相互作用的手征结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 L_μ-L_τ 模型是解释缪子反常磁矩等异常的良好候选者，但通常只包含矢量耦合。本文旨在构建一个包含轴矢量耦合的变体模型，使其既满足反常消除与可重整化要求，又能产生独特的实验信号，从而为区分不同 U(1)' 模型提供探针。

Method: 作者提出一个轴矢量 U_A(1)_{L_μ-L_τ} 模型，通过单态标量场和重矢量轻子经 universal-seesaw 机制产生 μ、τ 质量。在重矢量轻子、小轻-重混合且 m_s≥10 GeV 的参数限制下，可观测量主要依赖 (m_{A'}, g_X)。针对中微子三叉产生做了有限 μ 质量的精确计算，并结合纵模贡献、轫致辐射、不可见介子衰变、四缪子共振搜索等实验约束进行综合分析，同时讨论了未来缪子对撞机的角分布敏感性。

Result: 主要发现包括：(1) 在有限 μ 质量下，A' 修正的是轴矢量弱系数而非通常模型中的矢量系数；(2) A' 纵模增强 muon 轫致辐射并对 (g-2)_μ 产生负的支配性贡献；(3) 当 m_{A'}≫m_μ 时，矢量与轴矢量末态辐射速率几乎相同，可通速率匹配获得对撞机限制；(4) 在缪子对撞机上，仅总速率无法完全分辨相互作用结构，但前后不对称性对手征性有直接灵敏度。

Conclusion: 该轴矢量 L_μ-L_τ 模型在 (m_{A'}, g_X) 参数空间受到现有搜索的多重约束，但在未来缪子对撞机上可通过角分布测量（尤其是前后不对称性）有效区分其与通常矢量 L_μ-L_τ 模型的手征结构差异。

Abstract: We propose an anomaly-free and renormalizable axial $U_A(1)_{L_μ-L_τ}$ model and study its experimental signatures for $A'$ masses from the MeV scale to the TeV scale. The opposite charges of the left- and right-handed charged leptons forbid the usual muon and tau Yukawa interactions. Their masses are instead generated by a singlet scalar and heavy vector-like leptons through a universal-seesaw mechanism. We focus on heavy vector-like leptons, small light--heavy mixing, and $m_s\gtrsim10~\mathrm{GeV}$. In this limit, the observables considered here depend mainly on $(m_{A'},g_X)$, while the other model parameters are restricted by mixing and perturbativity. We confront this benchmark with current experimental searches. For neutrino trident production, our finite-$m_μ$ calculation shows that $A'$ modifies the axial weak coefficient, rather than the vector coefficient relevant to the usual $L_μ-L_τ$ model. The longitudinal mode enhances muon bremsstrahlung and gives a negative contribution to $(g-2)_μ$; the latter dominates over the scalar contribution in our benchmark. Combining these results with invisible meson decays and four-muon resonance searches, we summarize the phenomenological constraints in the $(m_{A'},g_X)$ plane. For $m_{A'}\gg m_μ$, vector and axial final-state-radiation rates become nearly identical, so the corresponding collider limits can be obtained by rate matching. At a future muon collider, the total rate alone does not fully resolve the interaction structure, whereas angular distributions, especially the forward--backward asymmetry in $μ^+μ^-\toτ^+τ^-$, retain direct sensitivity to chirality.

</details>


### [161] [From the November Revolution toward the Millennium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27279)
*Chris Quigg*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文是第四届粒子物理学史国际研讨会的主旨演讲，回顾了从J/ψ发现之前到1980年代初的粒子物理学发展历程，为后续研讨会报告提供历史背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为粒子物理学史研讨会提供开篇综述，梳理从J/ψ发现前史到1980年代初的关键发展脉络，为与会者的专题报告建立共同的历史背景和语境。

Method: 以主旨演讲的形式，按时间顺序回顾粒子物理学的发展历史，涵盖J/ψ发现之前的"史前史"阶段直至1980年代初期，并将这段历史与研讨会后续议程中的专题贡献相关联。

Result: 系统地概述了约1975年前至1980年代初期粒子物理学的重大进展，为研讨会提供了历史框架与学术铺垫。

Conclusion: 这篇主旨演讲为历时二十年的粒子物理学发展提供了历史综述，为后续各专题报告奠定了历史认知基础。

Abstract: The 4th International Symposium on the History of Particle Physics spanned two decades, from approximately 1980 to 2000. In this opening keynote lecture, I survey developments in particle physics from the prehistory of the $J/ψ$ discovery to the early nineteen-eighties. I offer context for the contributions that follow in the symposium program.

</details>


### [162] [Chiral soliton lattice in inhomogeneous magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27319)
*Tomas Brauner,Ramkumar Radhakrishnan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了QCD在手征孤子晶格（CSL）框架下对非均匀磁场的响应，发现弯曲磁场虽降低中性π凝聚的能量增益，但CSL型基态仍然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知均匀强磁场下QCD基态支持中性π介子组成的空间调制凝聚体（CSL）。然而实际物理场景（如重离子碰撞和中子星）中的磁场往往是非均匀的，因此需要探索非均匀磁场下是否仍存在类似的序基态。

Method: 使用QCD低能有效场论，在强磁场下仅保留中性π介子作为低能自由度。在π介子质量为零的极限下，对支持CSL型基态的磁场进行完整刻画，并对一类简单的无限族磁场解析求解CSL态。在非零质量情形下，采用能量泛函的全数值最小化方法。

Result: 在无质量极限下完整刻画了支持CSL的磁场条件，并对一类磁场解析求得CSL态。数值结果表明，弯曲磁场通常减少中性π凝聚带来的能量增益，但CSL型基态仍然可能存在。此外还绘制了均匀磁场和有限体积下QCD相图中CSL相的位置。

Conclusion: 主要结论是：尽管磁场弯曲会降低能量增益，非均匀磁场下CSL型基态依然可以存在，这为将CSL现象推广到重离子碰撞和中子星等实际场景提供了理论依据。

Abstract: It has been known that in sufficiently strong uniform magnetic fields, the ground state of quantum chromodynamics (QCD) supports a spatially modulated condensate of neutral pions, dubbed chiral soliton lattice (CSL). In this paper, we investigate whether a similar ordered ground state might exist when the external magnetic field is nonuniform, as appropriate for potential phenomenological applications, including heavy-ion collisions and neutron stars. To that end, we use the low-energy effective field theory of QCD, restricted to the neutral pions as the sole low-energy degrees of freedom in strong magnetic fields. In the limit of vanishing pion mass, we achieve a complete characterization of magnetic fields supporting a CSL-like ground state. Moreover, for a simple but infinite family of magnetic fields, we find the corresponding CSL state analytically. Going away from the massless limit requires full numerical minimization of the energy functional. Here we provide some sample numerical results, focusing on the qualitative differences as compared to the situations with a uniform magnetic field or a vanishing pion mass. The main conclusion remains unchanged: while bending the magnetic field typically reduces the energy gain due to the neutral pion condensation, a CSL-type ground state is still possible. As a byproduct of our work, we map the location of the CSL phase in the phase diagram of QCD in a uniform magnetic field and finite volume.

</details>

### [163] [Coordinate-Deletion Bundles from Composition Algebras: Hopf Defects, $KO$-Classes, and Real Projective Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26300)
*Marina Palaisti*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文通过在一个实合成除代数D（维数d=2,4,8，分别对应复数、四元数、八元数）上利用左乘矩阵构造了一个显式的秩d实向量丛E_D→RP^d，该丛在坐标点处的局部clutching映射恰好是（复/四元/八元）Hopf clutching映射。通过实K理论和Euler类相消，证明E_D同构于γ^⊕d（即超平面线丛的d重直和），从而给出了这些熟知丛的带标架缺陷的显式实现，特别是RP^8上γ^⊕8的半代数八元数实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的代数拓扑构造往往难以提供向量丛的显式、可计算的实现。作者希望利用实合成除代数（按Hopf-Hurwitz定理仅有的三种：C、H、Oct）的乘法结构，通过左乘矩阵作为转移数据，给出RN^d上标准向量丛γ^⊕d的显式构造和具体公式，尤其是八元数情形（d=8）在RP^8上缺乏此类显式实现。

Method: 在每个标准仿射开集上删除一个齐次坐标，将剩余坐标解释为D的元素，用对应的左乘矩阵作为转移函数构造秩d向量丛。该丛在远离d+1个坐标点处具有由矩阵决定的平凡化；相对该平凡化，每个被删除点处的局部clutching映射为S^{d-1}→SO(d), u↦L_u，分别是复、四元、八元Hopf clutching映射。利用同一组矩阵构造截面的非退化零点恰为坐标点，从而计算w(E_D)=1+x^d；再结合实K理论和Euler类相消证明E_D≅γ^⊕d。

Result: 证明了构造的向量丛E_D满足E_D≅γ^⊕d，其Stiefel-Whitney类为1+x^d。特别地，给出了RP^8上γ^⊕8的半代数八元数实现，具有九个指定的局部Hopf缺陷。此外还描述了任意域上的Pfister二次型对应的向量丛。

Conclusion: 该工作通过实合成除代数的左乘矩阵统一构造了RP^d上γ^⊕d的显式实现，将复、四元、八元Hopf clutching映射统一在一个框架中，并延伸到任意域上的Pfister二次型向量丛，为代数拓扑中众所周知的向量丛提供了具体的几何构造。

Abstract: Let \(D\in\{\C,\Hh,\Oct\}\) be a real composition division algebra of dimension \(d\in\{2,4,8\}\). We construct an explicit rank-\(d\) real vector bundle $ E_D\longrightarrow\RP^d$ by deleting one homogeneous coordinate on each standard affine chart, interpreting the remaining coordinates as an element of \(D\), and using the corresponding left-multiplication matrices as transition data. The bundle admits a matrix-determined trivialization away from the \(d+1\) coordinate points. Relative to this trivialization, each deleted point has local clutching map $S^{d-1}\longrightarrow\SO(d),\; u\longmapsto L_u$, which is respectively the complex, quaternionic, or octonionic Hopf clutching map.
  A section arising from the same matrices has exactly the coordinate points as nondegenerate zeros. Consequently, $w(E_D)=1+x^d$, and real \(K\)-theory together with Euler-class cancellation gives $E_D\congγ^{\oplus d}$. Thus the construction supplies explicit framed-defect realizations of these familiar bundles. In particular, it gives a semialgebraic octonionic realization of \(γ^{\oplus8}\) on \(\RP^8\) with nine specified local Hopf defects. We also describe the associated Pfister quadratic bundle over arbitrary fields.

</details>


### [164] [Local Bousfield classes via homological support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26876)
*Tobias Barthel,Natalia Castellana,Drew Heard,Beren Sanders,Changhan Zou*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究大张量三角范畴中对象A对应的A-局部范畴的同调与上同调Bousfield类。证明了同调支持可分类同调Bousfield类当且仅当A满足A-相对的同调检测性质，且此性质等价于A与同调剩余域的上积Bousfield等价。上同调Bousfield类由同调余支持分类则更强，等价于A-相对同调分层；该等价在绝对情形也是新的。此外，分层还等价于同调Bousfield类可分类且所有上同调Bousfield类都是同调的。应用到色同伦理论，分类了任何关于Morava K-理论上积的谱局部化的同调Bousfield类，并完全刻画了这些色局部化何时是相对同态分层的，给出了上同调Bousfield类非同调的新例子，回答Wolcott关于调和谱范畴的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大张量三角范畴中，理解Bousfield类的分类是稳定同伦论的核心问题。作者希望研究A-局部化范畴中的同调与上同调Bousfield类，揭示它们与同调检测及同调分层等结构性条件之间的精确联系，并应用于色同伦理论，回答包括Wolcott关于调和谱的开放问题。

Method: 论文通过引入A-相对的同调检测性质和A-相对的同调分层概念，建立它们与Bousfield类分类之间的等价关系。证明同调支持分类同调Bousfield类等价于A与同调剩余域上积的Bousfield等价；上同调Bousfield类的分类等价于A-相对同调分层。在色同伦理论中利用Morava K-理论局部化进行具体分析，通过构造具有空同调余支持的局部谱来产生上同调非同调的Bousfield类。

Result: (1) 同调Bousfield类的分类等价于A-相对同调检测性质，也等价于A Bousfield等价于同调剩余域的上积；(2) 上同调Bousfield类的分类（通过同调余支持）等价于A-相对同调分层，该等价即使在绝对情形也是新结果；(3) 分层还等价于同调Bousfield类可分类且每个上同调Bousfield类都是同调的；(4) 在色同伦理论中完整分类了Morava K-理论上积局部化的同调Bousfield类，并刻画了这些局部化的相对同调分层，构造了上同调非同调的Bousfield类的新例子，回答了Wolcott关于调和谱的问题。

Conclusion: 作者建立了同调/上同调Bousfield类分类与同调检测、同调分层之间的精确等价关系，深化了对张量三角范畴中Bousfield类结构的理解。色同伦理论中的具体应用不仅统一了已有结果，还提供了新的反例并解决了开放问题，表明分层条件在刻画上同调Bousfield类时起决定性作用。

Abstract: Given an object $A$ in a big tensor-triangulated category, we study the homological and cohomological Bousfield classes of the associated localization: the tensor-triangulated category of $A$-local objects. We show that the homological support classifies the homological Bousfield classes of the $A$-local category precisely when an $A$-relative form of the homological detection property holds. Moreover, we prove that this holds if and only if $A$ is Bousfield equivalent to a coproduct of homological residue fields. The analogous classification of cohomological Bousfield classes by homological cosupport is strictly stronger: it is equivalent to an $A$-relative form of homological stratification. This equivalence between stratification and the classification of cohomological Bousfield classes is new even in the absolute case. A further surprise is that stratification is also equivalent to the classification of homological Bousfield classes together with the statement that every cohomological Bousfield class is homological. Applied to chromatic homotopy theory, these results classify the homological Bousfield classes of any localization of spectra with respect to a coproduct of Morava $K$-theories. This covers many localizations of interest. We also completely characterize when such chromatic localizations are relatively homologically stratified. This yields new examples of cohomological Bousfield classes that are not homological. In particular, it answers a question of Wolcott concerning the category of harmonic spectra. Our examples are produced by exhibiting local spectra with empty homological cosupport.

</details>


### [165] [On orientability, Poincaré duality, and connectivity of GKM graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26999)
*Oliver Goertsches,Panagiotis Konstantis,Leopold Zoller*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了抽象GKM图的可定向性这一组合概念，并将其与Guillemin-Zara意义下的图同调联系起来。证明了GKM图的可定向性等价于有理（非等变）图同调代数的Poincaré对偶性，并由此证明了可定向GKM图在删除任意单个顶点后仍然连通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKM理论（Goresky-Kottwitz-MacPherson理论）为研究具有环面作用的流形的等变同调提供了组合模型，其中GKM图是核心工具。理解GKM图的可定向性及其与图同调的代数性质之间的关系，对于揭示图的组合结构与流形几何拓扑性质的深层联系具有重要意义。

Method: 作者从组合角度引入抽象GKM图的可定向性概念，通过建立可定向性与有理非等变图同调代数满足Poincaré对偶性之间的等价关系来展开分析。具体方法涉及对GKM图中顶点局部处的方向赋值结构进行分析，并将其与图同调代数的代数结构（尤其是Poincaré对偶性）进行对照和论证。

Result: 建立了GKM图可定向性与图同调代数Poincaré对偶性之间的等价性定理，并作为应用推论证明了可定向GKM图在删除任意单个顶点后依然保持连通性这一拓扑性质。

Conclusion: 该研究表明GKM图的组合可定向性完全被其有理图同调代数的Poincaré对偶性所刻画，这一等价关系揭示了图的组合结构与代数拓扑性质之间的深刻联系，并为可定向GKM图的结构性质（如顶点删除后的连通性）提供了新的代数视角和证明途径。

Abstract: We investigate a combinatorial notion of orientability for abstract GKM graphs and its connections to graph cohomology in the sense of Guillemin--Zara. In particular, we prove that orientability of the GKM graph is equivalent to Poincaré duality of the rational (non-equivariant) graph cohomology algebra. As an application, we prove that orientable GKM graphs remain connected after removing any single vertex.

</details>


### [166] [Persistence Meets Resistance: Doubling Down on Hardness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27269)
*Benedikt Kolbe,Tim Mayr*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限度量空间过滤的稳定不变量（持久同调）的近似计算问题。在n点度量空间（倍增维度增长为o(log n)且直径有界）中，提出了新颖的近似算法：在1参数情形下，通过重新审视贪心置换技术，首次推导出计算任何稳定条形码的加性ε-近似的线性时间算法；在多参数情形下，通过推导均匀采样的收敛率和近似质量界限，证明归一化测度双过滤（包括multicover和细分-Rips双过滤）的任何稳定不变量可以在常数时间（n中）内概率近似。此外，通过细粒度复杂性视角，证明矩阵秩计算可归约到Vietoris-Rips或Čech过滤条形码的近似计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久同调中的稳定不变量计算在实际应用中至关重要，但现有算法在高维或大规模数据上计算代价高昂。作者旨在填补1参数和多参数持久同调中近似计算的理论空白，特别是实现线性时间甚至常数时间的近似算法，以及深入理解该问题的计算复杂度下限。

Method: 1) 在1参数情形下，以新颖方式重新利用贪心置换（greedy permutations）技术，实现线性时间算法来计算任何稳定条形码的加性ε-近似。2) 在多参数情形下，推导均匀样本在收敛率和近似质量方面的理论界，将其方法扩展到归一化测度双过滤（如multicover和细分-Rips双过滤），实现时间与n无关（常数时间）的概率近似。3) 通过细粒度复杂性约简，将矩阵秩计算归约到Vietoris-Rips或Čech过滤条形码的近似，提供两种约简变体：适用于足够好的加性近似和任意常数因子乘性近似。

Result: 首次实现了1参数情形下任何稳定条形码的加性ε-近似的线性时间算法；在选定多参数过滤（归一化测度双过滤）中，任何稳定不变量的概率近似时间常数于n；算法运行时间的常数依赖于倍增维度、直径和成功概率。同时建立了从矩阵秩到条形码近似的约简，揭示了该问题的细粒度复杂度下界。

Conclusion: 该工作显著推进了持久同调中稳定不变量近似计算的理论边界，在特定度量空间条件下实现了线性甚至常数时间的近似算法，并通过细粒度约简揭示了近似条形码计算的内在复杂度下限。

Abstract: We present results on the approximate computation of stable invariants for filtrations of finite metric spaces in the context of persistent homology. We establish novel approximation algorithms in the setting of $n$-point metric spaces where the growth of the doubling dimension is in $o(\log n)$ and the diameter is bounded.
  In the $1$-parameter case, by revisiting known techniques (greedy permutations) in a new way, we derive the first linear-time algorithms for the problem of computing additive $\varepsilon$-approximations of any stable barcode.
  By deriving bounds on the convergence rate and the approximation quality of uniform samples, we extend the approach to selected multiparameter filtrations. We show that for normalized measure bifiltrations, including the multicover and subdivision-Rips bifiltration, any stable invariant can be probabilistically approximated in time constant in $n$. The constants in the running times of our algorithms depend on the doubling dimension, the diameter and the success probability.
  We further study the problem through the lens of fine-grained complexity and show that computing the rank of a matrix reduces to that of approximating the barcode of the Vietoris--Rips or Čech filtration. We present two variants of the reduction, one for sufficiently good additive approximations and the other for any constant factor multiplicative approximations.

</details>


### [167] [Koszul duality and Morita categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27450)
*Max Blans*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了谱中operad和cooperad的(∞,2)-范畴版本的Koszul对偶，即operad和双模的Morita范畴与cooperad和双余模的对偶Morita范畴之间存在等价关系，该结果涵盖了文献中各种形式的operadic Koszul对偶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的operadic Koszul对偶推广到更高级的范畴设置中，统一文献中已有的各种不同形式的Koszul对偶结果，使其在一个统一的(∞,2)-范畴框架下得到系统性刻画。

Method: 作者在谱（spectra）的背景下构造了operad与bimodule构成的Morita范畴，以及cooperad与bicomodule构成的对偶Morita范畴，并通过证明这两个(∞,2)-范畴之间的等价关系来建立高维Koszul对偶。

Result: 成功证明了operad和bimodule的Morita范畴与cooperad和bicomodule的对偶Morita范畴之间的(∞,2)-范畴等价，该结果统一并包含了文献中已有的各种operadic Koszul对偶形式。

Conclusion: 该研究为operadic Koszul对偶提供了一个统一的(∞,2)-范畴论框架，表明这一等价关系不是个别的现象，而是内在于范畴结构中的一般性对偶原理。

Abstract: We prove an $(\infty,2)$-categorical version of Koszul duality for operads and cooperads in spectra by showing that there is an equivalence between a Morita category of operads and bimodules and a dual Morita category of cooperads and bicomodules. This result subsumes various forms of operadic Koszul duality present in the literature.

</details>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [A $σ$-McKay theorem for $π$-separable groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26373)
*David Cabrera-Berenguer*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了G. Navarro提出的Hall σ-子群的McKay猜想类比，该结果同时推广了经典McKay猜想及其π-可解版本。具体地，对π-可解群G、素数p和σ=π∪{p}，证明了|Irr_{σ'}(G)|=|Irr_{σ'}(N_G(H))|，其中H是G的Hall σ-子群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: McKay猜想是有限群表示论中的一个核心猜想，涉及群与其子群正规化子的不可约特征标计数。Navarro提出了Hall σ-子群的McKay猜想类比，旨在将经典McKay猜想与π-可解版本的McKay猜想统一到更一般的框架中。证明这一猜想是该领域的重要进展。

Method: 作者针对π-可解群G，取σ=π∪{p}，其中p为素数。利用π-可解群中Hall σ-子群的存在性以及不可约特征标的σ'-部分（即Irr_{σ'}(G)）的结构性质，通过对特征标诱导、限制及相关同态关系的分析来建立G与N_G(H)之间σ'-不可约特征标的双射对应。

Result: 成功证明了对于π-可解群G，若H是G的Hall σ-子群（σ=π∪{p}），则|Irr_{σ'}(G)|=|Irr_{σ'}(N_G(H))|，即Navarro提出的Hall σ-子群McKay猜想类比成立。该结果同时推广了经典McKay猜想和π-可解版本的McKay猜想。

Conclusion: 该结果标志着McKay猜想在更一般框架下的重要进展，将经典McKay猜想与π-可解版本统一为Hall σ-子群的统一形式，为进一步研究有限群特征标与表示的相互作用提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We prove a Hall $σ$-subgroup analogue of the McKay conjecture for $π$-separable groups proposed by G. Navarro. This result simultaneously generalizes the classical McKay conjecture and its $π$-separable version. More precisely, if $G$ is $π$-separable, $p$ is a prime and $σ=π\cup\{p\}$, then it is known that there exists $H$ a Hall $σ$-subgroup of $G$. In this case, we prove that $|{\rm Irr}_{σ'}(G)|=|{\rm Irr}_{σ'}(\mathbf{N}_{G}(H))|$.

</details>


### [169] [Auslander-Reiten (n+2)-angles and local finiteness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26653)
*Jian He,Yu-Zhe Liu,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究$(n+2)$-角范畴的局部有限性问题。Zhou证明了当$n$为奇数时，若Auslander-Reiten $(n+2)$-角生成Grothendieck群的关关系，则范畴是局部有限的。对于偶数$n$的情形是否成立仍是一个开放问题。本文给出了该问题的一个部分肯定回答，通过建立一个充分条件使得相同蕴含关系对偶数$n$也成立，并证明该条件被一大类例子满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhou关于奇数维$(n+2)$-角范畴的局部有限性结果已被证明，但偶数$n$的相应命题是否成立仍然悬而未决。本文旨在部分解决这一开放问题，寻求使得相同结论对偶数$n$成立的充分条件。

Method: 本文通过建立一个新的充分条件，在该条件下证明当$n$为偶数时，若Auslander-Reiten $(n+2)$-角生成Grothendieck群的关关系，则范畴$\mathcal C$是局部有限的。此外，作者进一步验证该充分条件被广泛的一类例子所满足。

Result: 给出偶数$n$情形下原问题的一个部分肯定回答：即建立了使得Zhou的结论对偶数$n$成立的充分条件，并且证明了该条件对一大类具体例子均成立，说明该结果并非孤立的特例。

Conclusion: 本文为偶数$n$的$(n+2)$-角范畴局部有限性问题提供了部分肯定的解答，所建立的充分条件涵盖广泛的例子类，但该问题在一般情况下是否完全成立仍待进一步研究。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal C$ be an $(n+2)$-angulated category. Zhou proved that, when $n$ is odd, if the Auslander-Reiten $(n+2)$-angles generate the relations for the Grothendieck group of $\mathcal C$, then $\mathcal C$ is locally finite. Whether the corresponding statement remains valid for even $n$ is still open. In this paper, we give a partial affirmative answer to this problem by establishing a sufficient condition under which the same implication holds for even $n$. We further show that our sufficient condition is satisfied by a broad class of examples, thereby demonstrating that the result extends well beyond isolated cases.

</details>


### [170] [An enhanced Helgason-Johnson bound for $Sp(p, q)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26664)
*Zhan Ying,Chao-Ping Dong*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文改进了1969年Helgason-Johnson关于辛群Sp(p,q)的界，在不可约酉表示π为无穷维的假设下取得了更好的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Helgason-Johnson在1969年给出的界对于Sp(p,q)群上的相关分析问题是一个已知的重要结果，但在无穷维不可约酉表示的情形下，该界可能不是最优的，需要进一步改进。

Method: 在假设不可约酉表示π为无穷维的框架下，对Sp(p,q)群的Helgason-Johnson界进行精细化推导和估计，以获得更紧的界。

Result: 成功改进了Helgason-Johnson在1969年对Sp(p,q)群建立的界，在无穷维不可约酉表示的假设下得到了更优的结果。

Conclusion: 本文表明，在无穷维不可约酉表示的条件下，Sp(p,q)上的Helgason-Johnson界可以被进一步优化，为相关调和分析问题提供了更精确的工具。

Abstract: Under the assumption that the irreducible unitary representation $π$ is infinite dimensional, this paper improves the Helgason-Johnson bound in 1969 for $Sp(p, q)$.

</details>


### [171] [Unitary Shimura Correspondence for Complex Classical Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26795)
*Wan-Yu Tsai,Kayue Daniel Wong,Hongfeng Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个从复数群 G=SO_{2n}(C)（或 Sp_{2n}(C)）的容许 Harish-Chandra 模的 Grothendieck 群到其对应的自旋群 Spin_{2n}(C)（或 Spin_{2n+1}(C)）的真表示 Grothendieck 群的提升算子，并显式确定了任意特殊酉表示的象，证明这些表示是酉的且保持大类的酉性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究复数拓扑群与自旋群表示之间的关系，特别是希望建立一个提升算子，使得从经典群的 Harish-Chandra 模可以转移到自旋群的真表示，同时保持酉性等重要性质。

Method: 构造从 Grothendieck 群层面的提升算子，将 SO_{2n}(C) 与 Sp_{2n}(C) 的容许 Harish-Chandra 模映射到对应 Spin 群的真表示空间，并对特殊酉表示（special unipotent representations）显式计算其提升结果。

Result: 对所有特殊酉表示的提升进行了显式刻画；提升后出现的表示（若非零）是复数 Spin 群的真酉表示，且该提升算子在大量酉表示上保持酉性。

Conclusion: 该提升算子提供了一个从经典复数群到自旋群表示论的系统桥梁，且在一大类表示上保持酉性，为酉表示理论的研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct a lifting operator from the Grothendieck group of admissible Harish-Chandra modules of $G =\mathrm{SO}_{2n}(\mathbb C)$ (resp. $\mathrm{Sp}_{2n}(\mathbb C)$) to that of genuine representations of $\mathrm{Spin}_{2n}(\mathbb C)$ (resp. $\mathrm{Spin}_{2n+1}(\mathbb C)$). We determine the lift of the sum of special unipotent representations attached to any ${}^{\vee}\mathcal O \subseteq {}^{\vee}\mathfrak g$ explicitly. In particular, the representations occurring in these lifts, if nonzero, are genuine unipotent representations of complex Spin groups and are unitary. As a consequence, the lifting operator preserves unitarity on a large class of unitary representations.

</details>


### [172] [The Saito determinant for extended affine Weyl discriminant strata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26817)
*Andrea Brini,Karoline van Gemst*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩展仿射Weyl群反射表示在判别式任意分层上的Saito度量形式，计算了所有分层和Dynkin型的Saito行列式，并证明了其可被相关超平面排列的受限根q-模拟的乘积整除，同时推广了Weyl分母公式的因子分解和高维分层的结果，并为Antoniou-Feigin-Strachan关于Coxeter群的结果提供了q-模拟精细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Saito度量在奇点理论和Coxeter群理论中扮演重要角色，理解其在判别式不同分层上的限制形式有助于揭示超平面排列、Weyl群表示与q-模拟之间的深层联系。现有的Weyl分母公式因子分解和Antoniou-Feigin-Strachan的结果仅涵盖较低维数或一般情形，缺乏对高余维分层的完整刻画。

Method: 本文对扩展仿射Weyl群反射表示限制在判别式任意分层上的Saito度量进行系统研究，计算所有分层和Dynkin类型下的Saito行列式，并通过与超平面排列的受限根q-模拟建立联系来证明Saito行列式的可整除性。

Result: 证明了在所有分层和Dynkin类型下，Saito行列式可被相关超平面排列的受限根q-模拟的乘积整除。该结果同时将Weyl分母公式的因子分解推广到高余维分层，并为Antoniou-Feigin-Strachan关于Coxeter群的结果提供了q-模拟版本的精细化。

Conclusion: 本文系统地刻画了扩展仿射Weyl群判别式各分层上Saito度量的行列式结构，建立了与q-模拟受限根的深刻联系，从高余维角度统一并推广了经典因子分解公式，为后续研究提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: We investigate the form of the Saito metric on quotients of the reflection representation of an extended affine Weyl group, restricted to an arbitrary stratum of its discriminant. We compute the Saito determinant for all strata and Dynkin types and show that it is divisible by a product of q-analogues of restricted roots for the associated hyperplane arrangement. Our results simultaneously generalise the factorisation in the Weyl denominator formula to higher codimension strata, and provide q-analogue refinements of results of Antoniou-Feigin-Strachan for Coxeter groups.

</details>


### [173] [Noncommutative Cluster Varieties and Moduli Spaces of Local Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27284)
*Zachary Greenberg,Dani Kaufman,Merik Niemeyer,Anna Wienhard*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为每个简化根系R和标定曲面S构造了非交换簇簇A_{R,S}，统一推广了Fock-Goncharov、Li、Goncharov-Shen等人的簇簇理论。同时定义了一类称为Jordan分裂群的大类代数群，其李代数通过融合Tits-Kantor-Koecher构造与分裂李代数构造而建立。论文还给出了A_{R,S}的Jordan代数点，它们参数化G局部系统，并推广了Berenstein-Fomin-Zelevinsky的簇代数到双R-Bruhat胞腔上的非交换簇结构，最后讨论了正结构及其在实代数群中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有簇簇理论（Fock-Goncharov、Goncharov-Shen等）针对不同根系和曲面分别发展，缺乏统一的非交换推广。同时，Jordan代数与簇簇之间的联系尚未被系统探索。本文旨在通过引入Jordan分裂群的概念，将非交换簇簇与Jordan代数统一起来，并推广到更广泛的代数群和根系统分类中，同时为双R-Bruhat胞腔提供非交换簇结构。

Method: 本文方法包括：(1) 为每个简化根系R和标定曲面S构造统一的非交换簇簇A_{R,S}；(2) 定义Jordan分裂群G，其李代数通过融合Tits-Kantor-Koecher构造（针对单个Jordan代数）与分裂李代数构造而构建；(3) 利用根系R对李代数进行分级，证明这些分级通常由标准抛物子代数p_Θ诱导，并通过仅依赖简单根系子集Θ的条件分类R-分级对(G,Θ)；(4) 定义A_{R,S}的Jordan代数点，参数化带边界装饰的G-局部系统；(5) 当S为圆盘时，用这些点参数化装饰旗形构型，在双R-Bruhat胞腔上建立非交换簇结构；(6) 在Jordan代数为形式实的条件下定义正结构和正半群。

Result: 本文成功地：(1) 构造了统一的非交换簇簇A_{R,S}，涵盖多个已知簇簇理论作为特例；(2) 定义了Jordan分裂群并完成了R-分级对(G,Θ)的分类；(3) 证明了A_{R,S}的Jordan代数点参数化G-局部系统，在圆盘情形下参数化装饰旗形构型；(4) 在双R-Bruhat胞腔上建立了非交换簇结构，推广了Berenstein-Fomin-Zelevinsky的经典簇代数；(5) 证明了实代数群中具有正结构的对(G,Θ)恰好是Guichard-Wienhard意义下可正结构的，并给出了正旗形构型和正表示的代数证明。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入Jordan分裂群和非交换簇簇A_{R,S}，在Jordan代数理论与簇簇理论之间建立了深刻联系，统一并推广了多个已知的簇簇构造。论文不仅给出了系统的R-分级对分类，还为双R-Bruhat胞腔提供了非交换簇结构，并通过正结构将代数方面的理论（Jordan代数、分裂群）与几何方面的应用（正旗形构型、正表示、G-局部系统）联系起来，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this article, we construct noncommutative cluster varieties, $\mathcal{A}_{R,S}$, for each reduced root system $R$ and marked surface $S$ simultaneously generalizing the cluster varieties of Fock-Goncharov, Li, Goncharov-Shen, Berenstein-Retakh, Goncharov-Kontsevich, and our previously introduced polygonal cluster algebras. Additionally, we define a large class of algebraic groups, we call Jordan split groups. Given a reduced root system $R$ and a family of Jordan algebras, the Lie algebra for $G$ is constructed by unifying the Tits-Kantor-Koecher construction for a single Jordan algebra with the construction of a split Lie algebra.
  The notion of Jordan split groups is closely related to a grading of its Lie algebra by the root system $R$. We show that these gradings are usually induced by a choice of standard parabolic subalgebra $\mathfrak{p}_Θ$ and we classify $R$-graded pairs $(G,Θ)$ via a condition depending only on the subset $Θ\subset Δ$ of the set of simple roots. Next, we define Jordan algebra points of $\mathcal{A}_{R,S}$ which parameterize $G$-local systems on $S$ with boundary decoration related to cosets $G/U_Θ$ when $G$ is Jordan split of type $R$. When $S$ is a disk, points of $\mathcal{A}_{R,S}$ parameterize configurations of decorated flags. We use this to give noncommutative cluster structures on the double $R$-Bruhat cells of $G$, generalizing the cluster algebras of Berenstein-Fomin-Zelevinsky. When each Jordan algebra is formally real, we say that $G$ has a positive structure with respect to $Θ$. This defines a positive semigroup in $G$. For real algebraic groups, the pairs $(G,Θ)$ which have positive structures are exactly those which admit a positive structure as defined by Guichard-Wienhard and we give algebraic proofs of many of the properties of positive configurations of flags and of positive representations.

</details>
